Method: 提出BENCH2ROBUST框架，将无故障工具基准转化为可控随机环境，通过场景控制的可行性设定，使事件显式地要求智能体进行重试、切换或停止。同时研究两种互补干预：一是结构化的运行时恢复上下文（贝叶斯工具记忆BTM），二是课程控制的强化学习（RL）。

Result: 在4个家族的7个模型和两个多轮基准家族上进行评估，发现工具失败导致了近乎普遍的鲁棒性差距。在保留的Retail任务上，BTM无需重训练即可将鲁棒性提升高达16.8个百分点；RL则学习到互补的恢复行为，在无推理时BTM的情况下依然有效。两者结合在注入条件下达到40.8-45.5%，同时保持无故障性能不下降。

Conclusion: 鲁棒工具使用受益于将环境特定的恢复知识（BTM）与学习到的恢复行为（RL）相结合，两者具有互补性，可为工具失败场景下的智能体提供更全面的恢复能力。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents are commonly trained and evaluated in environments where tool calls succeed reliably, yet deployed tools can fail transiently, persistently, or silently. Robust recovery therefore requires more than repeated retries: an agent may need to retry the same path, switch to an alternative, or recognize that no viable path remains. We present BENCH2ROBUST, a framework that converts failure-free tool-use benchmarks into controlled stochastic environments with scenario-controlled solvability, where episodes explicitly require retrying, switching, or stopping after available paths are exhausted. We use BENCH2ROBUST to study two complementary interventions: structured runtime recovery context through Bayesian Tool Memory (BTM), and curriculum-controlled reinforcement learning. Across 7 models from 4 families and two multi-turn benchmark families, tool failures produce a near-universal robustness gap. On held-out Retail tasks, BTM improves robustness by up to 16.8 percentage points without retraining, while RL learns complementary recovery behavior that remains beneficial without inference-time BTM. Combining the two reaches 40.8-45.5% under injection while preserving failure-free performance. These results suggest that robust tool use benefits from combining environment-specific recovery knowledge with learned recovery behavior.

</details>


### [231] [Claim-Level Reliability Assessment for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11994)
*Sen Xu,Wei Wang,Shixi Liu,Jixin Min,Yingwei Dai,Zhibin Yin,Yirong Chen,Junlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"声明级证伪"（claim-level falsification）的测试时扩展原则，并实例化为CLR（Claim-Level Reliability Assessment）框架。CLR无需训练，将推理轨迹压缩为关键声明进行定向验证，利用"构造正确解与证伪错误解的天然不对称性"来重新分配测试时计算资源，在四个LLM和四个推理基准上以更少token显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法主要依赖增加更多候选解采样，但整条轨迹评估常因常规token的信号稀释而掩盖关键错误；同时，在模型能力固定下生成完全正确的解本身就十分困难。因此需要一种能聚焦于决策关键点、利用证伪不对称性的高效验证机制。

Method: CLR包含三个核心环节：(1) 将每条推理轨迹压缩为紧凑的决策关键声明集，隔离逻辑锚点；(2) 采用语义证伪替代构造验证，利用"只要找到一个决定性缺陷即可推翻错误声明"这一不对称性；(3) 通过定向搜索负面证据压缩高置信错误轨迹的生存空间，并以非线性可靠性评分抑制错误共识，最终实现无需训练的测试时计算再分配。

Result: 在四个LLM和四个推理基准上，在匹配计算预算条件下，CLR普遍优于pass@1和自一致性（self-consistency）。在GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25上，CLR比pass@1提升27.15个百分点，自一致性准确率从77.50%提升至82.19%，同时节省37.0%的token。

Conclusion: 声明级证伪作为测试时扩展的新原则是有效的：相比于盲目增加采样，将计算投入定向验证能更高效地提升推理可靠性。CLR验证了"证伪比构造更容易"这一不对称性在实际推理场景中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We propose claim-level falsification as a principle for test-time scaling and instantiate it through Claim-Level Reliability Assessment (CLR), a training-free framework that reallocates test-time compute from additional solution sampling to targeted verification. Since whole-trace evaluation often obscures decisive errors due to signal dilution from routine tokens, CLR condenses each reasoning trace into a compact set of decision-critical claims, thereby isolating its logical anchors. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent difficulty of generating entirely correct solutions under fixed model capabilities, CLR shifts the focus to semantic falsification. This approach exploits a fundamental asymmetry between solution construction and claim refutation. Constructing a valid solution requires a flawless reasoning path, whereas refuting an incorrect claim requires identifying only a single decisive flaw. This targeted search for negative evidence systematically compresses the survival space of high-confidence incorrect traces, effectively suppressing erroneous consensus via nonlinear reliability scoring. Across four LLMs and four reasoning benchmarks under matched budgets, CLR generally improves upon pass@1 and self-consistency. On GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25, for instance, CLR exceeds pass@1 by 27.15 percentage-points and raises self-consistency accuracy from 77.50\% to 82.19\% with 37.0\% fewer tokens.

</details>


### [232] [CTBench: Evaluating Troubleshooting Capabilities of AI Agents in Realistic Telecom Network Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12002)
*Xingyu Yan,Tingting Dai,Antonio De Domenico,Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Changchang Li,Bowen Liu,Kun Jiang,Mengjie Zhang,Dingcheng Shan,Jing-Cheng Pang,Chenwei Wu,Sijie Wu,Lianying Chao,Haoran Cai,Jiantao Ye,Xubin Li,Simon Mark Lucas,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CTBench，一个用于评估代理人（agent）在部分可观测电信环境中进行网络故障诊断的公开基准，聚焦于根因分析和路径恢复两大任务，并采用专家构建的指标体系评估最终答案与诊断证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估未能准确建模真实网络特征，也未能在包含多种供应商、设备、协议和接口的部分可观测电信环境下评估智能体（agent）的表现，缺乏对电信运维工程师能力的系统性评测基准。

Method: CTBench由领域专家构建任务，每个任务均带有丰富的元数据（包括黄金证据步骤）。基准采用专家驱动的评估指标，同时评价最终答案和诊断证据链，并与多种代表性harness-model组合进行实验。

Result: 最先进的智能体在路径恢复任务的端点识别上表现良好，但在根因分析上普遍欠佳；尤其难以处理接口状态、链路层、服务管理及其他运维故障。此外，即使智能体给出看似正确或合理的最终答案，也经常缺乏符合运维实践的证据支撑。路径恢复总体上更消耗资源，但更大的资源消耗并不必然带来更好的诊断效果。

Conclusion: 当前SOTA智能体尚未达到称职电信运维工程师的水平，尤其在证据驱动的诊断能力上存在显著短板，CTBench为这一方向提供了可量化的评测基础。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly considered for automating network operations and maintenance, where engineers must diagnose network faults, optimize configurations to enhance services, and reduce operational costs while acting under strict constraints. However, existing evaluations fail to accurately model real network characteristics or assess agents under partially observable telecom environments with diverse vendors, devices, protocols, and interfaces. In this paper, we introduce CTBench, a public benchmark for assessing whether an agent behaves like a competent telecom troubleshooting engineer. CTBench focuses on root cause analysis and path restoration. Each task is constructed by experts and annotated with rich task metadata, including golden evidence steps. CTBench uses expert-grounded metrics that evaluate both final answers and the diagnostic evidence. Experiments with representative harness-model combinations show that state-of-the-art agents perform very well at identifying endpoints in path-restoration tasks but, more generally, underperform in root cause analysis. In particular, agents struggle with interface state, link-layer, service-management, and other operational faults. Most importantly, even when agents produce plausible or correct final answers, they often fail to provide the evidence-grounded diagnoses required in operational practice. Our results further show that path restoration is generally more resource expensive, yet larger resource usage does not necessarily translate into better diagnosis.

</details>


### [233] [Mechanist: AI as a Scientific Instrument for Discovering the Mechanisms of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12036)
*Mengru Wang,Junfeng Fang,Shuofei Qiao,Zhenqian Xu,Haoming Xu,Haoxiong Wang,Shumin Deng,Linyi Yang,Zhixiang Cui,Xin Xu,Yunzhi Yao,Buqiang Xu,Fei Shen,Haozhe Luo,Yunxiang Wei,Ningyu Zhang,Julian McAuley,Tat Seng Chua,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Mechanist，一个将AI作为科学仪器自主发现AI智能机制的智能体系统。它整合了约1.3万篇可解释性论文的知识图谱和涵盖26个领域4300万篇论文的多学科数据库，并策划了32种基础分析方法库。Mechanist在生成机制假设和执行实验方面优于Claude Code和现有AI科学家系统，展示了从发现模型行为到解释和控制AI模型的完整路径，包括发现跨模态安全风险转移、构建信念机制理论以及将机制洞察转化为实际干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型取得了显著成功，但其能力背后的机制和潜在风险仍不为人知。随着AI开发速度加快且日益自动化，机制探索仍以手工为主，模型能力与我们对它的理解和控制之间的差距不断扩大。

Method: Mechanist是一个智能体系统，构建了可解释性聚焦的知识图谱（约13,000篇论文）并与涵盖26个领域的4,300万篇论文的多学科数据库集成，同时策划了32种机制分析、因果干预和验证的基础方法库。

Result: 相比Claude Code和现有AI科学家系统，Mechanist能生成更有价值的机制假设并更可靠地执行实验。它展示了从发现模型行为到解释和控制AI模型的进展：发现跨模态安全风险转移、构建信念机制理论（模型如何表征世界知识、形成信念、推断他人信念及其在预训练中的涌现），并将机制洞察转化为实际干预来提升模型性能以及引导科学基础模型生成具有指定属性的DNA序列。

Conclusion: Mechanist展示了AI作为科学研究仪器可以自主推进机制发现，缩小AI能力与人类理解之间的差距，并通过机制洞察实现从发现到解释再到控制AI模型的完整闭环。

Abstract: AI models have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet the mechanisms underlying their capabilities and the risks they may pose remain poorly understood. As AI development becomes faster and increasingly automated, mechanistic exploration remains largely manual, widening the gap between what models can do and our ability to understand and control them. To bridge this gap, we introduce Mechanist, an agentic system that uses AI as a scientific instrument for the autonomous discovery of mechanisms underlying AI intelligence. To support autonomous mechanistic discovery, we construct an interpretability-focused knowledge graph of approximately 13,000 papers and integrate it with a multidisciplinary database of 43 million papers spanning 26 fields. We further curate a library of 32 foundational methods for mechanism analysis, causal intervention, and validation. Compared with Claude Code and existing AI-scientist systems, Mechanist generates more valuable mechanism hypotheses and executes experiments more reliably. Mechanist also demonstrates a progression from discovering model behaviors to explaining and controlling AI models. Specifically, Mechanist first uncovers a counterintuitive safety risk in scientific laboratories, showing that unsafe traits can transfer across modalities through apparently safe training data. Mechanist then develops a mechanism theory of belief, revealing how models represent world knowledge, form beliefs, infer the beliefs of others, and how these mechanisms emerge during pretraining. Finally, Mechanist translates these mechanistic insights into practical interventions that improve model performance across diverse scenarios and steer scientific foundation models toward generating DNA sequences with specified properties.

</details>


### [234] [Graph-Structured Rubrics: Compiling Rubrics into Typed Evaluation Graphs for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12097)
*Xi Chen,Jie Mu,Mo Xuan,Qun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图结构化评分标准（Graph-Structured Rubrics, GSR），将评分标准编译为与响应无关的类型化评估图，通过命名端口组合评判节点和变换/归约/门控算子，并使用任务特定的Readout输出映射将唯一汇节点转换为分数或偏好。在GPT-OSS-120B上，GSR在四个点式数据集上比Prometheus风格评分提升0.62-6.75个百分点，并在两个偏好基准上达到最高的端到端配对准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于评分标准的评估器通常将评分标准视为提示上下文或扁平标准，仅指定评判内容，但将标准组合方式隐式化，即使自然语言规则中明确说明了组合方式。这导致（1）评分标准的组合逻辑被忽略或处理不当，（2）复杂的组合规则（如条件、归约、门控）难以准确执行，（3）评估流程缺乏结构化的可验证性。

Method: GSR方法包括：(1) 将评分标准编译为响应无关的"类型化评估图"，在观察响应之前完成编译；(2) 图中包含三类节点——标准节点（elicits judgments）、变换/归约/门控算子（通过命名端口组合评判）、以及任务特定的Readout输出映射（将唯一汇节点转换为分数或偏好）；(3) 编译过程拒绝格式错误或类型不兼容的图；(4) 点式评估先生成各维度评判再执行图聚合，配対式评估复用同一图，每个标准对每个候选做出一次评判。(5) 使用GPT-OSS-120B作为基础模型。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-120B下，GSR在四个点式数据集上相比Prometheus风格评分，精确分数一致性提升了0.62至6.75个百分点；在两个偏好基准上，在原生平局与弃权策略下取得了数值上最高的端到端配对准确率。

Conclusion: GSR通过将评分标准明确建模为结构化评估图，使得评分标准中的组合逻辑得以显式地编译和执行，从而提升了LLM评估的准确性和可靠性。该工作表明，将评估流程结构化（而非仅作为扁平上下文）是提升自动评估性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluators commonly treat rubrics as prompt context or flat criteria: they specify what to judge but leave criterion composition implicit, even when natural-language rules state it. We introduce Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR), which compiles a rubric into a response-independent typed evaluation graph before observing responses. Criterion nodes elicit judgments; transformation, reduction, and gating operators compose them through named ports; and a task-specific output mapping, termed Readout, converts the unique sink into a score or preference. Compilation rejects malformed or type-incompatible graphs. Pointwise evaluation judges rubric dimensions separately before graph aggregation; pairwise evaluation reuses the graph with one judgment for each candidate under every criterion. Under GPT-OSS-120B, GSR improves exact score agreement by 0.62--6.75 percentage points over Prometheus-style scoring on four pointwise datasets and achieves the numerically highest end-to-end pairwise accuracy on two preference benchmarks under native tie and abstention policies.

</details>


### [235] [GUIDE: Governed Unified Intelligence for Document-to-Artifact Generation in Enterprise Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12133)
*Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Mohammadreza Sediqin,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GUIDE是一个基于共享版本化规则库的多智能体框架，通过六个专业化智能体（解析、VLM提取、一致性检查、评估、人机回退、工件生成）以及模式验证的智能体间合约和端到端溯源追踪，解决企业指南文档的异构多模态处理难题。在120份真实企业文档上实现96%文档成功率，提取3,896条规则（71.4%自动批准），生成812个部署就绪工件，单文档处理时间从2-3天缩短至40-125分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业指南文档具有异构性和多模态特点（文本、复杂表格、嵌入图片混杂），现有LLM/VLM系统存在幻觉内容、表格结构退化问题，且缺乏超越提取环节的受治理工作流（验证和工件生成），导致企业需人工处理，每份文档耗时2-3天。

Method: 提出GUIDE框架：基于共享版本化规则库构建多智能体系统，采用模式验证的智能体间合约和端到端溯源追踪。六个专门智能体分别负责解析、VLM驱动提取、一致性检查、评估、人机回退（HITL）升级和面向角色的工件合成。

Result: 在120份真实企业指南文档上评估：文档处理成功率达96%，提取3,896条规则（其中71.4%可自动批准），产出812个部署就绪工件，单份文档处理时间降至40-125分钟。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过受治理的多智能体编排显著提升企业指南文档处理的自动化水平和效率，将处理时间从2-3天缩短至1-2小时以内，并通过HITL回退和版本化规则库保障了提取结果的可靠性与合规性。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents are heterogeneous and multimodal, combining narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images. Existing LLM and VLM systems face hallucinated content, table structure degradation, and lack governed workflows extending beyond extraction to validation and artifact generation. This leaves enterprises to perform this manually, consuming 2-3 days per document. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, a governed multi-agent framework built on a shared versioned rule store with schema-validated inter-agent contracts and end-to-end provenance tracking. Six specialized agents handle parsing, VLM-driven extraction, consistency checking, evaluation, human-in-the-loop (HITL) escalation, and persona-tailored artifact synthesis. Evaluated on 120 real-world enterprise guideline documents, GUIDE achieves 96% document success, extracts 3,896 rules with 71.4% auto-approved, produces 812 deployment-ready artifacts, and reduces turnaround to 40-125 minutes per document.

</details>


### [236] [Who Thinks Best Depends on How Long You Let Them: Budget-Dependent Rankings in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12150)
*Rodrigo Guedes de Souza,Alison R. Panisson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"大语言模型排名在不同推理条件下稳定"的假设，通过改变token生成预算（64至4096）在三个推理基准上评估四个模型，发现3-19%的样本呈现非单调行为，模型排名在不同预算下发生反转，并设计了预算感知路由来捕捉模型互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型评估通常假设模型在不同推理条件下排名稳定，但token生成预算（最大可生成token数）这一推理条件可能会显著影响模型表现。作者希望验证这一假设是否成立，并探讨预算对模型排名、模型互补性及路由策略的影响。

Method: 作者在七个预算级别（64至4096）下，对四个模型在三个推理基准上进行了共56,476次推理评估。通过控制截断效应分析非单调行为的普遍性，使用McNemar检验模型排名反转的显著性，进行Oracle分析量化模型互补性，并设计预算感知路由器来检验预算特征在模型选择中的作用。

Result: 研究发现：(i) 3-19%的样本在增加预算时准确率反而下降，且该现象具有模型特异性（跨模型重叠仅6-14%）；(ii) 所有基准上模型排名均随预算反转（p<0.01）；(iii) Oracle分析显示模型互补性最高可达+27.8个百分点；(iv) 预算感知路由可捕获Oracle差距的14.1%，预算特征在域内有效（+1.6至+5.7pp）但具有域特异性且损害迁移（-1.2pp）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明应建立预算条件化的评估协议，即评估LLM时应明确并控制token生成预算，因为模型排名和性能在不同预算条件下并非稳定不变。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of large language models assumes stable model rankings across inference conditions. We challenge this assumption by varying the token generation budget, i.e., the maximum tokens a model may produce, across seven levels (64--4,096), evaluating four models on three reasoning benchmarks (56,476 inferences). We report four findings: (i) 3--19% of items exhibit non-monotone behavior (accuracy decreasing with more budget), even after controlling for truncation, and this phenomenon is model-specific (cross-model overlap: 6--14%). (ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [237] [How to Spend Your Oracle Budget: Practical Guidance for Protein Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12192)
*Aleksandra Kalisz,Jack Simons,Krisztina Sinkovics,Noam Ghenassia,Shikha Surana,Henry Moss,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了多种面向蛋白质结构预测模型的外部oracle引导方法（FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N和新增的O3），在不同oracle预算下评估了它们在两个蛋白质靶标（1CLL和9EEH）上的表现，发现没有单一方法在所有预算下都占优：O3在低预算下最有效，而FK-steering和DPO在高预算下表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质结构预测的基础模型在部分靶标上仍不可靠，需要外部oracle来标记和纠正失败，但生物学oracle成本高昂，预算有限。现有引导方法（FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N）在预算分配策略上各不相同，却缺乏系统性的比较来指导方法选择，这是本文试图填补的空白。

Method: 将新提出的Optimisation Over Outputs (O3)方法（在生成模型的潜在子空间中应用现成优化器）扩展应用到蛋白质结构预测任务中，并与FK-steering、DPO和Best K-of-N方法进行系统benchmark。在calmodulin (1CLL)和E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH)两个蛋白质靶标上进行评估，比较不同oracle预算下的性能表现。

Result: 实验发现不存在在所有预算和oracle组合下都一致占优的单一方法。具体而言，O3在低oracle预算下表现最优，而FK-steering和DPO随着预算增加性能有所提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个关于oracle预算感知引导的实用参考，并将发现提炼为针对在现实oracle预算约束下工作的从业者的可操作建议，帮助他们在不同预算条件下选择最合适的引导方法。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [238] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体工作流，在版本控制的规格说明下由三个提示词特化的智能体角色大规模地现代化改造遗留Fortran代码，并利用领域固有的精确验证预言机（位级能量复现）作为安全委派的边界。在GAMESS量子化学软件包的两电子积分例程转换（56,448行，12个源文件，225个子程序）中，所有文件通过51项测试验证，612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留Fortran代码的现代化改造面临数量巨大的问题：单个变换虽属常规，但代码库体量庞大，且计算科学领域中大量此类工作因规模而被搁置。作者希望探索是否能通过智能体工作流将这项工作在工业规模上委派出去，并测量这种委派能到达的安全边界。

Method: 提出一个智能体工作流：三个提示词特化的智能体角色（Claude Code）在隔离的工作树中运行，在智能体自身编写和修订的版本控制规格说明下工作，人类持有少量门控。安全性通过领域固有的精确验证预言机保证——即GAMESS的位级能量复现测试。该工作流应用于将GAMESS（拥有48年历史的量子化学软件包）的固定格式Fortran 77代码转换为自由格式Fortran 2008，覆盖12个源文件、56,448行和225个子程序。实验跨越四代Claude模型。

Result: 全部12个源文件通过了51项测试的验证（49项标准GAMESS测试加2项额外计算），在612次测试运行中化学相关的差异数量为零，且每个文件也通过了Jenkins持续集成测试。十二位小数的偏差即被视为失败而非漂移。

Conclusion: 安全委派的边界恰好位于验证预言机能看到的范围之内。当领域存在精确的验证标准（如位级能量复现）时，智能体工作流可以在生产规模上可靠地执行遗留代码现代化任务，且人类只需持有少量关键门控。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [239] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍VAKRA基准，一个涵盖62个领域、8,000+可执行API的智能体评测基准，任务涵盖三种难度递增的设置（多样API交互、多跳结构化推理、多源推理与自然语言工具使用策略约束），评测发现即使最佳模型在单跳任务上仅达70.4%，在组合式API上降至50-51%，且推理深度增加导致性能下降超50%，策略约束下的不可回答查询上最差仅2.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将结构化API推理与文档知识检索分开评测，而企业环境中部署的智能体必须同时处理两者，缺乏统一评测框架。

Method: 构建VAKRA基准，包含8,000+可执行API、62个领域，设计三种难度递增的任务设置（多样API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、带自然语言工具使用策略约束的多源推理）。通过重新执行预测的工具调用对实时API进行正确性验证，允许多种有效路径，并使用固定的ReAct框架隔离模型能力与智能体架构。

Result: 前沿与开源模型评测显示：最佳模型仅达70.4%（单跳端点式任务），组合式API上降至50-51%；推理深度增加导致性能下降超50%；策略约束问询暴露严重缺陷（不可回答查询上低至2.4%）。轨迹分析表明失败集中在语言介导推理环节（实体消歧、跨源对齐），而非工具调用机制本身。

Conclusion: VAKRA揭示现有智能体在语言介导推理（实体消歧与跨源信息对齐）方面的系统性短板，指出该方向是提升企业级API智能体性能的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [240] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用检索增强生成（RAG）和大语言模型（LLM）从系统描述中自动构建动态主机逻辑（DML）模型并将其表示为知识图谱（KG-DML）的框架，将该方法从先前的小规模系统扩展至更大更复杂的系统，并在退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却注入系统上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML提供了一种将功能目标与底层结构元素关联起来的层级化系统行为表示框架，但传统上DML的构建依赖专家对技术文档的解读，这种人工方式限制了其在复杂系统中的可扩展性，因此需要自动化构建方法。

Method: 该框架利用检索增强生成（RAG）和大型语言模型（LLM）作为使能工具，从系统描述中自动构建KG-DML。模型构建跨越DML层级进行针对性检索，同时保留功能依赖关系和显式逻辑关系。验证采用多层级评估方法，考察各层的精确率与召回率、逻辑门一致性和整体结构完整性。

Result: 将该框架应用于退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却注入系统，重复运行显示一致的构建结果。结果表明自动化KG-DML构建能将技术文档转化为可用于诊断和可靠性分析的可执行功能模型。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建框架是可行的，能够将技术文档有效转化为可执行的功能模型，支持诊断推理、安全评估、向上故障传播和向下依赖追踪等应用，为复杂系统的功能建模提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Semantic Error Control Coding with Foundation Models for Future Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11551)
*Chentao Yue,Gaoyang Pang,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出语义错误控制编码（SECC）框架，通过基础模型学习源数据的统计与语义结构，将其作为先验信息融入信道编码与解码过程，使编码冗余优先分配给语义重要的内容，并在译码端提升可靠性估计、候选搜索和纠错性能，在AWGN信道上获得数分贝的编码增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信道译码将所有信息序列视为等概率，仅依赖信道观测和码结构，未利用源数据的统计或语义结构。虽然源压缩旨在去除冗余，但实际编码后仍残留大量结构信息未被利用。多模态数据（文本、语音、图像）具有丰富的统计和语义依赖，基础模型可学习这些结构以改进信道译码性能。

Method: 提出SECC框架，将基础模型对源内容的先验概率作为语义源先验，在编码端将其用于引导冗余分配至语义重要的内容；在译码端用于改进可靠性估计、候选搜索和错误检测/纠正。信道码保持代数结构，其约束确保基础模型给出的语义建议符合码结构。将设计方法分为三类，并在文本源上实现代表性方案。

Result: 在AWGN信道上相比传统译码获得数分贝的编码增益，错误率低于常规近似界（normal approximation bound）。

Conclusion: SECC展示了利用语义先验提升信道译码性能的可行性，指出了信息论与语义通信结合的方向，并识别出若干开放性挑战供后续研究。

Abstract: Classical channel decoding typically treats all information sequences as equally likely and relies primarily on the channel observations and code structure, without exploiting statistical or semantic structure in the source data. Although source compression is designed to remove redundancy, practical source coding can leave substantial residual structure that conventional channel decoders do not exploit. Modern multimodal data sources, including text, speech, and images, exhibit rich statistical and semantic dependencies that foundation models can learn and exploit to improve channel decoding. This article introduces semantic error control coding (SECC), which seamlessly integrates the semantic structure of the source into encoding and decoding through a foundation model. The semantic source prior, represented by the model's a priori probability of the source content, directs code redundancy toward semantically important content at the encoder, and improves reliability estimation, candidate search, and error detection/correction at the decoder. The channel code keeps its algebraic structure, and its constraints ensure that the semantic suggestions from the foundation model comply with this structure. We describe the SECC framework, classify its design methods into three approaches, and demonstrate representative schemes on text sources. The demonstrated schemes show several decibels of coding gain over conventional decoding on AWGN channels, and reach error rates below the normal approximation bound. Finally, we identify several open challenges.

</details>


### [242] [An AoI-oriented Time-Frequency Distributed Access Mechanism in Wireless Sensor Networks with Spectrum Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11599)
*Jingwei Liu,Fang Liu,Wing Shing Wong,Yuan-Hsun Lo,Chung Shue Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模随机激活传感器网络中低开销接入设计的信息新鲜度问题，提出了一种年龄感知的确定性时分频分分布式接入（D-TFDA）机制，通过周期性令牌分配实现无碰撞传输，并利用一维离散时间马尔可夫链和线性规划实现长期平均信息年龄（AAoI）的最优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频谱分割技术使多个正交资源上的并发上行传输成为可能，但现有研究对大规模随机激活传感器网络中兼顾低开销接入设计和信息新鲜度的问题关注不足。

Method: 提出D-TFDA机制，结合集中式配置与分布式操作，采用周期性的基于令牌的时频结构提供无碰撞且可预测的传输机会；通过一维离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）对传感器稳态进行建模以刻画AAoI；利用令牌分配模式的周期结构性质识别AoI等价令牌簇以缩减搜索空间，将化简后的问题表述为线性规划（LP），并设计AAoI最优搜索算法及低复杂度的拍卖启发式算法。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提出的AAoI分析框架的准确性，证明了令牌分配算法的有效性，并显示D-TFDA通过避免碰撞和利用异质传感器-资源传输可靠性，相比优化的随机接入基线方案实现了显著更低的AAoI。

Conclusion: D-TFDA机制能够在低运行时开销下实现无碰撞传输并有效优化信息新鲜度，为大规模随机激活传感器网络的接入设计提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of spectrum-division techniques enables concurrent uplink transmissions over multiple orthogonal resources, yet low-overhead access design with effective information freshness remains insufficiently studied for large-scale randomly activated sensor networks. In this paper, we apply the age of information (AoI) to measure information freshness and propose an AoI-efficient deterministic time-frequency distributed access (D-TFDA) mechanism. D-TFDA combines centralized configuration and distributed operation through a periodic token-based time-frequency structure, which provides sensors with collision-free and predictable transmission opportunities without considerable run-time overhead. We develop an analytical framework to characterize the long-term average AoI (AAoI) by exploiting the periodicity of the token assignment pattern and modeling the steady local state of each sensor with a one-dimensional discrete-time Markov chain (DTMC). We further reveal structural properties of the token assignment pattern and identify AoI-equivalent token clusters, which substantially reduce the search space of the AAoI-optimal token allocation problem. Based on this structure, we formulate the reduced problem as a linear programming (LP) problem and develop an AAoI-optimal search algorithm, together with an auction-inspired heuristic algorithm of lower complexity. Simulation results validate the proposed AAoI analysis, demonstrate the effectiveness of the token allocation algorithms, and show that D-TFDA achieves substantially lower AAoI than optimized random access baselines by avoiding collisions and exploiting heterogeneous sensor--resource transmission reliability.

</details>


### [243] [Sparse Rotatable Arrays (SRA): Unifying Array Aperture and Antenna Directivity for Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11666)
*Ailing Zheng,Qingqing Wu,Xiyuan Liu,Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种基于用户服务分组的感知稀疏旋转天线阵列架构，通过群体级几何的低复杂度配置，联合优化稀疏孔径分配、旋转天线朝向和发射波束成形，以最大化所有用户中最小的信号与干扰加噪声比（SINR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏阵列可降低大规模MIMO硬件成本但存在严重旁瓣和栅瓣泄漏；旋转天线（RA）能提供方向性泄漏抑制，但频繁对准瞬时用户位置会带来可观的控制开销。实际中用户长期分布往往非均匀（紧凑热点与离散用户并存），这使得基于群体级几何的低复杂度RA配置成为可能，从而需要一种群体感知的稀疏RA架构。

Method: 将用户组织为服务组，激活的RA划分为组特定稀疏子阵列；联合优化稀疏孔径分配、RA朝向和发射波束成形；利用RA诱导的组间泄漏抑制与稀疏孔径诱导的组内正交化实现近似解耦的组级波束成形结构；开发结构化的两层低复杂度算法，内含闭式投影组中心RA朝向规则，结合分析引导初始化、采样多起点天线分配搜索和基于二分法的二阶锥规划波束成形。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的设计紧密逼近全共享且朝向优化的基准方案，同时显著优于紧凑型和全向稀疏阵列方案。

Conclusion: 群体感知的稀疏旋转天线阵列设计能够在保持接近最优性能的同时大幅降低控制复杂度和硬件成本，验证了基于群体级几何的低复杂度RA配置在实际非均匀用户分布场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse arrays reduce the hardware cost of extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output systems while preserving a large effective aperture, but may suffer from severe sidelobe and grating-lobe leakage. Rotatable antennas (RAs) provide directional leakage suppression, whereas frequent boresight adaptation to instantaneous user locations incurs considerable control overhead. In practice, long-term user distributions are often non-uniform, with compact hotspots coexisting with scattered users, which enables low-complexity RA configuration based on group-level geometry. Motivated by this observation, we propose a group-aware sparse RA architecture in which users are organized into service groups and the activated RAs are partitioned into group-specific sparse subarrays. We maximize the minimum signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) among all users by jointly optimizing the sparse-aperture allocation, RA orientations, and transmit beamforming. We show that RA-induced inter-group leakage suppression and sparse-aperture-induced intra-group orthogonalization jointly yield an approximately decoupled group-wise beamforming structure, providing physical guidance for group-wise antenna-number allocation and non-periodic sparse-position initialization.
  Guided by these insights, we develop a structured low-complexity two-layer algorithm that embeds a closed-form projected group-center RA orientation rule into the sparse-aperture allocation and beamforming design. The algorithm combines analysis-guided initialization, sampled multi-start antenna-allocation search, and bisection-based second-order cone programming for beamforming. Numerical results show that the proposed design closely approaches the fully shared and orientation-optimized benchmarks while substantially outperforming the compact and omnidirectional sparse-array schemes.

</details>


### [244] [Complete characterization of the differential spectrum of a Niho type power function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11757)
*Nian Li,Xi Xie,Rui Xu,Yi Yu,Xiangyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域F_{2^{2m}}上Niho型幂函数F(x)=x^{s(2^m-1)+1}的差分性质，建立了一般性的差分谱刻画框架，并针对s=(2^k+1)^{-1} mod (2^m+1)的特殊情形完全确定了其差分谱，构造了新的无穷族局部差分4-一致幂函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Niho指数的幂函数在序列设计、编码理论和密码学中有重要应用，其差分性质（特别是差分谱和差分均匀性）是评估其密码安全性（如抵抗差分密码分析）的关键指标。此前对这些函数的差分性质缺乏完全刻画。

Method: 首先通过Walsh谱建立了F(x)具有至多三个非零差分谱值的一般性特征描述；然后针对s=(2^k+1)^{-1}(mod 2^m+1)且gcd(k,m)=e的情形，通过精细分析有限域上特定方程的解数，分两种情形（gcd(2^k-1,2^m+1)=2^e+1和gcd(2^k-1,2^m+1)=1）分别证明了局部差分均匀性并完全确定了差分谱。

Result: 当gcd(2^k-1,2^m+1)=2^e+1时，F(x)是局部差分2^e-均匀的；当gcd(2^k-1,2^m+1)=1时，F(x)是局部差分(2^{2e}-2^e)-均匀的，且两种情形的差分谱均被完全确定。由此构造了新的无穷族局部差分4-均匀幂函数。

Conclusion: 该研究完全刻画了Niho型幂函数F(x)=x^{s(2^m-1)+1}（其中s满足特定条件）的差分性质，为密码学中寻找具有良好差分特性的S盒函数提供了新的理论支撑和构造方案。

Abstract: Power functions with Niho exponents have attracted considerable attention due to their important applications in sequence design, coding theory, and cryptography. This paper investigates the differential properties of Niho type power functions of the form $F(x)=x^{s(2^m-1)+1}$ over $\mathbb{F}_{2^{2m}}$ with $2\leq s\leq 2^m$. We first establish a general characterization of the differential spectrum of $F(x)$ having at most three nonzero values via its Walsh spectrum. Focusing subsequently on the case $s=(2^k+1)^{-1} \pmod{2^m+1}$ where $\gcd(k,m)=e$, we employ a refined analysis of the number of solutions to certain equations over finite fields. Specifically, it is proved that $F(x)$ is locally differentially $2^e$-uniform when $\gcd(2^k-1,2^m+1)=2^e+1$ and locally differentially $(2^{2e}-2^e)$-uniform when $\gcd(2^k-1,2^m+1)=1$, and their differential spectra are completely determined. These results completely characterize the differential properties of this family and yield new infinite families of locally differentially $4$-uniform power functions.

</details>


### [245] [Proof-Valid Caching under Premise Erasures: Local Structural Limits and Shared-Workload Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11782)
*Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究语义透明缓存系统（缓存对象必须是前提基的逻辑结论）在独立前提擦除下的可靠查询恢复问题，提出了查询局部投影定理和精确残叶定律，证明单查询设计等价于加权部分路径拦截；对共享负载引入语义模块并推导精确可靠性定律，证明共享模块缓存在精确模块路由和同质成本下最优，而一般推导DAG上的最优选择在深度2时是NP完全的；与编码基准对比显示MDS奇偶校验缓存最多次优一个数据包，叶级透明性存在与擦除率成反比的一阶开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语义透明缓存系统中，缓存对象的有效性依赖于前提基的可推导性。当独立前提发生擦除时，缓存的可靠性会受到影响。现有工作缺乏对这类系统中查询恢复可靠性的精确刻画，尤其是擦除如何通过推导图传播到查询、以及缓存设计如何在最优性与复杂度之间权衡。

Method: 在确定性规范见证（canonical-witness）框架下，作者证明了查询局部投影定理和精确残叶定律——恢复失败当且仅当被擦除的基叶保留一条无缓存路径到查询。据此将单查询设计转化为加权部分路径拦截问题。对共享负载引入语义模块概念，分别推导联合误差和最大误差准则下的精确可靠性定律。通过MDS编码缓存作为基准进行对比分析，并利用Datalog实例和蒙特卡洛模拟验证理论。

Result: 共享模块缓存在精确模块路由和同质成本下精确最优；一般推导DAG上的最优模块选择在深度2即为NP完全。与编码基准相比，MDS奇偶校验缓存最多仅比最优次优一个数据包。叶级透明性产生与1/擦除率成正比的一阶开销；共享模块将该缩放因子乘以模块到叶成本比除以被保护叶数。

Conclusion: 本文为推导结构化的内容提供了函数修正存储（function-correcting storage）在随机擦除下的精确对应物，并对最大可恢复性（maximal recoverability）给出了精确的分布量化。理论通过Datalog实例和蒙特卡洛模拟得到验证，为语义透明缓存的可靠性和最优设计提供了系统的分析基础。

Abstract: We study reliable query recovery under independent premise erasures in semantically transparent caching systems, where every cached object must be a logical consequence of the premise base. Recovery succeeds only when the query remains derivable from surviving premises and the cache. Under a deterministic canonical-witness regime, we prove a query-local projection theorem and an exact residual-leaf law: recovery fails exactly when an erased base leaf retains a cache-free path to the query. Single-query design becomes weighted partial path interception. For shared workloads, we introduce semantic modules and derive exact reliability laws under joint and maximal-error criteria. The shared-module cache is exactly optimal under exact module routing and homogeneous costs, whereas optimal selection in general derivation DAGs is NP-complete at depth two. Against a coded benchmark recovering workload-relevant leaf payloads, MDS parity caching is optimal up to one packet. Leaf-only transparency incurs a first-order overhead inversely proportional to the erasure rate; shared modules multiply that inverse-erasure-rate scaling by the module-to-leaf cost ratio divided by the number of protected leaves. A Datalog instance and Monte Carlo checks illustrate the theory. For derivation-structured content, the results provide exact stochastic-erasure counterparts of function-correcting storage and an exact distributional quantification of maximal recoverability.

</details>


### [246] [AFDM-ISAC With Fractional Delay-Doppler Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11998)
*Shaohua Li,Cunhua Pan,Hong Ren,Ruizhe Wang,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对AFDM（仿射频分复用）波形的ISAC（集成感知与通信）系统，提出了一种联合估计角度与连续值延迟-多普勒参数的CC-NOMP（耦合坐标牛顿化正交匹配追踪）估计器，解决了离散延迟-多普勒网格导致的能量泄漏、峰值偏移以及AFDM啁啾引起的延迟-多普勒耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM作为高机动ISAC的候选多载波波形，其传感应用中需要精确的角度、延迟和多普勒估计。然而，目标延迟和多普勒为连续值，离散网格表示会导致DAFT域的能谱泄漏和峰值偏移；且AFDM啁啾会引起DAFT域响应中的延迟-多普勒耦合，产生不沿归一化轴对齐的局部脊线，传统离散网格方法难以有效处理。

Method: 首先从分数DAFT域响应构建变换域稀疏感知模型；在此基础上提出CC-NOMP估计器，利用AFDM诱导的耦合坐标参数化主导局部脊线，结合耦合坐标牛顿细化、安全更新、耦合对齐延迟细化以及循环多目标细化，实现对角度、连续归一化延迟和归一化多普勒的联合估计。同时推导了确定性Cramér-Rao界和主阶复杂度分析。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在连续值离网格目标参数条件下，CC-NOMP在延迟和多普勒估计误差底限方面优于所比较的基线方法，同时保持了相当的角度估计精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CC-NOMP方法有效解决了AFDM-ISAC中由连续值参数和啁啾耦合导致的DAFT域估计难题，实现了高性能的联合角度-延迟-多普勒估计，为高机动场景下的AFDM感知提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) is a promising chirp-based multicarrier waveform for high-mobility integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). Accurate angle, delay, and Doppler estimation is essential for AFDM sensing. Since target delays and Doppler shifts are generally continuous-valued, representing them on a discrete delay--Doppler grid causes energy leakage and peak displacement in the discrete affine Fourier transform (DAFT) domain. The AFDM chirp also induces delay--Doppler coupling in the DAFT-domain response. The resulting DAFT-domain matching-score surface exhibits a local ridge that is not aligned with the normalized-delay and normalized-Doppler axes. To address these issues, this paper investigates joint estimation of angle and continuous-valued delay--Doppler parameters for a colocated AFDM-ISAC sensing architecture. A transform-domain sparse sensing model is formulated from the fractional DAFT-domain response. Based on this model, a coupled-coordinate Newtonized orthogonal matching pursuit (CC-NOMP) estimator is developed. CC-NOMP uses the AFDM-induced coupling coordinate to parameterize the dominant local ridge. It combines coupled-coordinate Newton refinement with safeguarded updates, coupling-aligned delay refinement, and cyclic multi-target refinement to estimate angle, continuous normalized delay, and normalized Doppler. A deterministic Cramér--Rao bound and a dominant-order complexity analysis are also derived. Simulation results with continuous-valued off-grid target parameters show that CC-NOMP achieves lower delay and Doppler error floors than the considered baselines while maintaining comparable angle-estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [247] [Secure Coverage Enhancement in Aerial Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface-Assisted High-Speed Train Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12046)
*Changzhu Liu,Ruisi He,Bo Ai,Yong Niu,Zhu Han,Gongpu Wang,Haoxiang Zhang,Jiahui Han,Zhangdui Zhong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了空中可重构智能表面（ARIS）辅助的高速列车（HST）通信系统的物理层安全（PLS）问题。通过联合优化基站（BS）处的主动波束成形和ARIS处的相位偏移，以最大化加权和保密速率。提出了一种基于块坐标下降法的联合优化算法，分别使用逐次凸逼近技术设计主动波束成形，并使用交替方向乘子法更新ARIS相位偏移。仿真结果表明该算法收敛速度快，且相比现有方法能实现更高的保密速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速列车通信系统面临窃听等严重安全威胁，而现有技术难以有效解决物理层安全问题。将可重构智能表面（RIS）集成在无人机上形成的空中可重构智能表面（ARIS）技术有望提升无线网络性能，但其在物理层安全方面的应用仍存在独特挑战，缺乏系统的保密速率优化方案。

Method: 提出最大化加权和保密速率的联合优化问题，同时约束BS发射功率和ARIS反射系数的单位模量。采用块坐标下降法（BCD）将问题分解为两个子问题：主动波束成形设计利用逐次凸逼近（SCA）技术进行最优求解；ARIS相位偏移优化则采用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）进行高效迭代更新。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的联合优化算法具有快速的收敛特性，并且相比现有文献中的方法能够实现更高的保密速率。

Conclusion: ARIS技术结合所提出的联合优化算法可有效提升高速列车通信系统的物理层安全性，为未来智能交通通信中的安全传输提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: High-speed trains (HSTs) have become a prominent means of transportation, requiring high data rates and reliable communication services for HST passengers. However, the wireless channels in HST communication systems are susceptible to various security threats, including eavesdropping. Addressing these security concerns is therefore of critical importance. One promising technology for enhancing security is the integration of a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) on an unmanned aerial vehicle, referred to as an aerial reconfigurable intelligent surface (ARIS). This technology offers significant potential for improving wireless network performance, though it also introduces unique challenges in terms of physical layer security (PLS). This paper investigates the PLS of ARIS-aided HST communication systems. A problem of maximizing the weighted sum secrecy rate is formulated by jointly optimizing the active beamforming at the base station (BS) and the phase shift at the ARIS, subject to constrains on the BS transmit power and the unit modulus of the ARIS reflecting coefficient. To address this problem, a joint optimization algorithm is proposed using the block coordinate descent method. Specifically, the problem is decomposed into two subproblems: active beamforming design and ARIS phase shift optimization. The active beamforming is optimally designed via the successive convex approximation technique, while the ARIS phase shift is efficiently updated using the alternating direction method of multipliers technique. Simulation results demonstrate the rapid convergence of the proposed algorithm, which achieves a higher secrecy rate compared to existing methods in the literature.

</details>


### [248] [The Optimal Discounting Parameter of the Power Prior under Predictive Log-Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12159)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在预测对数损失准则下，为Ibrahim-Chen幂先验中的指数a0给出了闭式最优解：a0* = d/(2N0·D̄0 + d)，即最优借用有效样本量遵循谐振律1/E* = 1/N0 + 2D̄0/d。该结果对多项分布精确成立，并可推广至光滑参数族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ibrahim和Chen提出的幂先验通过将历史似然提升到幂次a0 ∈ [0,1]来整合历史数据，但指数a0的选取一直是一个悬而未决的开放问题。

Method: 在预测对数损失（predictive log-loss）框架下，推导最优幂指数a0*的闭式表达式。考虑d维参数模型、规模为N0的历史样本，以及历史分布与当前数据生成分布之间的平均KL散度D̄0。

Result: 最优幂指数为a0* = d/(2N0·D̄0 + d)。等价地，最优借用有效样本量满足谐振律1/E* = 1/N0 + 2D̄0/d：数据兼容时完全合并；存在差异时借用信息被限制在d/(2D̄0)个观测以内。该结果对多项分布精确，并可推广到光滑参数族。此外，该定律为自适应借用提供了基准，解释了归一化幂先验的退化现象，并表明数据子集化或指数衰减并不比正确折扣的常数指数更优。

Conclusion: 该研究为幂先验的指数选择提供了理论最优解，建立了借用有效样本量和谐振律这一直观法则，为贝叶斯历史数据借用方法提供了理论基准，并澄清了已有方法（如归一化幂先验退化）的相关理论问题。

Abstract: The power prior of Ibrahim and Chen incorporates historical data into a Bayesian analysis by raising the historical likelihood to a power $a_0 \in [0, 1]$. The choice of the exponent has remained an open question. This paper gives a closed-form answer under the predictive log-loss. For a model with $d$ parameters, a historical sample of size $N_0$, and average Kullback--Leibler divergence $\bar{D}_0$ between the historical and current data-generating distributions, the optimal exponent is $a_0^{*} = d/(2 N_0 \bar{D}_0 + d)$. Equivalently, the optimally borrowed effective sample size obeys the harmonic law $1/E^{*} = 1/N_0 + 2\bar{D}_0/d$: compatible data are pooled in full, and any difference caps the borrowed information at $d/(2\bar{D}_0)$ observations. The result is exact for multinomial data and extends to smooth parametric families. The law benchmarks adaptive borrowing, explains the reported degeneracy of the normalized power prior, and shows that neither subsetting the data nor decaying the exponent improves on the correctly discounted constant.

</details>


### [249] [Lossy Compression, Realism, and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12222)
*Yassine Hamdi,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了率失真感知（RDP）权衡这一新兴研究领域，揭示了带真实性约束的有损压缩与限速率通信下的分布式协调问题之间的深层联系，两者在信息论表征、对公共随机性的依赖及分析工具（如软覆盖引理）上高度相似，并探讨了将批处理批评器和算法真实性等新范式迁移至协调问题的研究前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典率失真理论追求最小化失真，但最小化失真往往产生感知上不令人满意的重建结果（如模糊图像、过度平滑纹理和伪影）。这促使了带真实性约束的压缩研究兴起，形成了三分量的率-失真-感知（RDP）权衡。本文旨在为该新兴领域提供易读的综述，并深入揭示其与分布式协调问题之间未被充分认识的基本联系。

Method: 本文采用统一的综述视角，首先介绍率-失真-感知（RDP）权衡的核心框架，随后对比分布式协调问题，指出两者在强分布匹配表述下具有几乎一致的信息论表征，都依赖公共随机性（CR）实现最优性能，并共享软覆盖引理等分析工具。文章还综述了形式化真实性的最新进展（包括批处理批评器和算法真实性），并提议将这些范式迁移至协调问题中以催生新问题。

Result: 文章表明，在强分布匹配框架下，率-失真-感知压缩与速率受限分布式协调在数学结构和信息论极限上高度同构，两者均需要公共随机性来达到最优性能，且均可通过软覆盖引理等工具进行分析。此外，批处理批评器和算法真实性等新近提出的真实性形式化方法有望跨界适用于协调问题。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了一个统一视角，将压缩中的真实性约束与分布式协调问题联系起来，不仅揭示了两个经典问题之间深层的理论关联，还为未来研究指明了方向——将新的真实性形式化范式从压缩领域迁移到协调领域，从而持续产生新的开放问题。

Abstract: Classical rate-distortion theory characterizes the fundamental limits of lossy compression under fidelity constraints, but minimizing distortion often yields perceptually unsatisfying reconstructions - blurry images, over-smoothed textures, and unnatural artifacts. This has motivated a growing body of work on compression with realism constraints, which require reconstructions to be statistically indistinguishable from natural signals, giving rise to the three-way rate-distortion-perception (RDP) trade-off. This paper provides an accessible overview of this emerging area and reveals deep connections to another fundamental problem: distributed coordination under rate-limited communication. Under strong distribution matching formulations, both problems lead to nearly identical information-theoretic characterizations, both require common randomness (CR) for optimal performance, and both rely on similar analytical tools such as the soft covering lemma. Beyond a unifying perspective, we survey recent developments in formalizing realism, including batched critics and algorithmic realism, and propose to transfer such paradigms to coordination - illustrating how the connection continues to generate new problems.

</details>


### [250] [Satellite Infrastructure Sharing: Orbit-Structured Stochastic Geometry Modeling and Connectivity Analysis in Heterogeneous Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12265)
*Chang-Sik Choi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分析框架，用于评估多个低轨（LEO）卫星运营商之间基础设施共享的可行性与性能。通过球形Cox点过程建模轨道结构与卫星分布，并结合球形Cox-Boolean模型刻画地球表面的覆盖区域，推导了连接概率、连接数量以及下行信号特征（如最近服务距离、总接收功率和SIR分布）的闭式表达式。仿真验证表明，该方法能够解析地表征轨道几何如何决定覆盖、连通性和干扰，并揭示异构星座中覆盖重叠带来的内在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限卫星资源下日益增长的普适连接需求背景下，多个独立运营的LEO星座通常缺乏预定义的轨道协议而处于非协调部署状态。现有方法难以刻画这种异构、非协调的星座共存场景，因此需要一种能够同时建模轨道结构与卫星空间分布的解析框架，来评估基础设施共享的可行性和收益。

Method: 采用球形Cox点过程联合生成轨道结构和卫星位置，以刻画独立运营星座的非协调共存；为每颗卫星随机分配通信范围以反映不同运营商的覆盖能力差异；在覆盖区域建模上，利用球形Cox-Boolean模型捕捉各卫星球形足迹在地球表面的空间重叠；在此基础上推导关键性能指标的闭式表达式。

Result: 推导了连接概率、连接数量、最近服务距离、总接收信号功率以及干扰受限场景下信干比（SIR）分布等关键指标的理论闭式表达式。系统级仿真验证了分析结果的准确性，表明该框架能够为轨道几何如何影响覆盖、连通性和干扰提供易于处理的刻画。

Conclusion: 该解析框架为异构LEO星座共存下的基础设施共享提供了可行的分析工具，揭示了覆盖重叠所带来的固有性能权衡，为卫星基础设施共享的工程决策与系统设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper develops an analytical framework to evaluate the feasibility and performance of satellite infrastructure sharing among multiple low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite operators. Motivated by the growing demand for universal connectivity under limited satellite resources, the proposed model captures uncoordinated deployments where independently operated constellations coexist without predefined orbital agreements. To describe such heterogeneous configurations, the spherical Cox point process is employed to jointly generate orbital structures and satellites. Then, each satellite is further assigned a random communication range, reflecting variations in coverage capability across operators. The overall coverage region is modeled through a spherical Cox-Boolean model that captures the spatial overlap of individual satellite spherical footprints on Earth. Using the proposed framework, the feasibility and benefits of satellite infrastructure sharing are mathematically analyzed, and closed-form expressions are derived for key performance metrics such as the connection probability, connection number, and downlink signal characteristics including the nearest serving distance, total received signal power, and the signal-to-interference ratio (SIR) distribution in the interference-limited regime. The analytical results, validated through system-level simulations, provide a tractable characterization of how orbital geometry governs coverage, connectivity, and interference, and reveal the inherent trade-offs induced by coverage overlap in heterogeneous satellite constellations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FarSky是一种基于生成式框架的太阳辐照度预测方法，通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的共享潜在表示，再结合潜在扩散模型生成未来潜在状态，实现概率性辐照度预测，在独立测试集上相比现有方法提升预测技能最高达11个百分点，并显著改善了爬坡事件的检测能力（F1分数超过60%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏发电并网至关重要。全天空成像仪虽能提供高分辨率云层观测，但现有深度学习方法多为确定性预测，难以预测爬坡事件（ramp events），缺乏概率性预测能力，限制了预测的可靠性。

Method: FarSky包含两个核心组件：1) 多任务自编码器，学习图像重建与辐照度估计的共享潜在表示；2) 潜在扩散模型，基于近期观测生成未来潜在状态，并通过随机采样获得内在的概率性预测。利用西班牙Almería太阳平台多年ASI数据集进行模型开发与训练。

Result: 在两个独立测试数据集上，FarSky在确定性与概率性预测方面均取得最佳性能，预测技能较现有方法提升最高11个百分点；爬坡事件检测F1分数超过60%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将生成模型与任务感知的潜在空间耦合相结合，能够有效提升太阳辐照度预测的准确性、概率性表达能力及爬坡事件检测能力，展示了该框架在光伏预测领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [252] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现商用AI检测器无法区分AI辅助编辑与全文LLM生成，且检测结果存在系统性偏差：合规的轻度编辑被误报率为64-80%，未修改原文误报率9-15%（非STEM显著高于STEM），人类化改写后逃逸率超过96%。检测分数不应作为学术不端的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高校使用商用AI检测器维护学术诚信，但检测器在概念上无法区分AI辅助编辑与完全由LLM生成的草稿，可能将两者都视为学术不端。本文旨在量化这一政策失败，检验检测器在代理人工/人类标签下的实际误报与漏报情况，并评估其对合规AI辅助使用者的不公惩罚风险。

Method: 对已发表的英文摘要进行受控研究，覆盖四个学科领域，对比2013-2015与2023-2025两个时间段。在tau=0.50的代理人工/AI标签设定下，采用Pangram和GPTZero检测器，设置轻度"仅润色摘要"编辑作为合规AI辅助的代理，并使用Undetectable AI进行人类化改写测试逃逸率，同时分析长token密度和学术词汇表密度与检测分数的关联。

Result: 轻度AI润色编辑被标记率高达64-80%；未修改的2023-2025原文被标记率为9-15%，非STEM领域的误报率显著高于STEM（p<0.001）；高分与长token及学术词汇表密度相关而非作者意图本身；经Undetectable AI人类化后漏检率超过96%（检测率<4%）。

Conclusion: 诚实的AI辅助编辑比借助人类化工具规避检测面临更高的制裁风险，这种"诚实者受罚"的倒挂激励表明检测器评分不应作为学术不端的独立证据，需要重新审视现有AI检测驱动的学术诚信政策。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [253] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin是一个Rust编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来定义和求解最小化问题，包含广泛的求解器目录并对约束提供一流支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学计算中的一个基础任务，广泛应用于模型拟合、仿真校准、机器学习训练和工程参数优化等场景。需要一个统一且一致的工具来表述和求解这些优化问题。

Method: 设计并实现了一个名为Basin的Rust数值优化库，提供一致的问题表述和求解接口，内置丰富的求解器目录，并原生支持约束条件。

Result: Basin库为Rust用户提供一个统一、一致的方式来进行数值优化，涵盖多种求解器和对约束的一流支持。

Conclusion: Basin作为Rust生态中的数值优化库，通过统一的API和丰富的求解器选择，降低了数值优化问题的使用门槛，提升了求解的便利性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [254] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC刀具磨损预测中的应用，将刀具轨迹分布到模拟客户端以模拟联邦学习场景，结果表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习且显著优于本地客户端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNC加工中的刀具磨损预测对产品质量和工艺可靠性至关重要，但工业环境中加工数据分布在不同机器、站点或组织之间，且存在数据共享限制，导致机器学习方法难以直接应用。

Method: 采用联邦学习框架实现分布式刀具磨损预测，无需传输原始操作数据；将刀具轨迹分布到模拟客户端代表联邦学习场景，并与集中式参考模型和本地客户端基线模型进行对比评估。

Result: 联邦学习性能与集中式学习相当，且显著优于本地客户端模型。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够支持分布式CNC制造环境下的协作式刀具磨损预测。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [255] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"决策-资源"视角下的终端对称性（terminal symmetry），将顺序构造任务中的终端对称性分解为传输-细化-验证（transport-refine-certify）三阶段，并据此提出方法\method{}，通过状态受限的过程秩、残差秩刷新与序数秩交来实现终端对称性的复用，在CAD装配、小型程序和精确填充等任务中显著提升任意时刻AUC并降低验证器成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构造任务在完成时呈现精确对称性，但其执行过程是定向且依赖历史的。现有方法未将这种"终端对称性"作为可复用的决策资源加以利用，缺乏从决策视角系统化分解该特性并转化为可操作算法框架的理论。

Method: 提出transport-refine-certify分解原则：(1) transport——过程证据提供方向性，将结构跨等价结果传输；(2) refine——实现状态证据在转移后刷新当前决策相关性；(3) certify——固定验证器认证执行。\method{}实例化该原则：采用episode固定的传输过程结构、状态受限的过程秩、状态依赖的残差秩（在接受的转移后刷新），以及序数秩交操作——其top-k集合恰为两个提议前缀的并集。秩交在前缀覆盖下提供完成保证，并在对应前缀信息模型下达到紧的最坏情况验证器查询界。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充任务中，状态级刷新分别将任意时刻AUC提升最多6.77、21.75和8.68个百分点。在来自官方GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除episode上，\method{}在所有三种规模下均达到最低的平均封顶验证器代价，优于GRN和CDGS风格规划器。状态信号还能跨聚合与调度器组织迁移。两状态构造预测了转移后严格的动态-静态分离。

Conclusion: 终端对称性可以被系统化地转化为顺序构造中的可复用决策资源。通过传输-细化-验证的分解与实例化，该方法不仅在多项任务上取得显著性能提升，还提供了理论上的完成保证与查询复杂度边界，为处理具有终端对称性的定向构造任务提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [256] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种上下文感知的质量-多样性进化强化学习控制器（CQD-ERL），用于热带水冷式冷水机组及其风侧的监督控制。该控制器不是收敛到单一的标量化策略，而是维护一个产品档案库，包含由数据驱动的运行上下文（每日天气和负荷模式的聚类）以及上下文不变的behavior descriptor联合索引的专业策略。该档案库由免梯度的进化算子和共享经验回放的软演员-评论家（SAC）策略梯度算子共同填充，且每次动作在执行前都经过确定性安全护罩的过滤。控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近度和湿度约束的两层降阶环境中训练，并在全年回测中与ASHRAE指南36基线进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习控制器通常收敛到单一标量化策略，难以应对暖通空调（HVAC）领域中多变的天气和负荷条件。本文的动机在于通过质量-多样性范式维护一组专门化的策略，以更好地适应热带建筑冷水机组系统复杂多变的运行环境。

Method: CQD-ERL采用产品档案（product archive）维护由两个算子共同填充的策略库：一个用于探索多样行为的免梯度进化算子，以及一个共享经验回放的SAC策略梯度算子。档案库以数据驱动的运行上下文（天气与负荷聚类）和上下文无关的行为描述符联合索引。每个动作在执行前通过确定性安全护罩进行过滤，确保安全性。训练环境为两层降阶模型，模拟新加坡商业建筑的潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近和湿度约束。

Result: 控制器在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行比较评估，具体节能效果和性能指标在摘要中未给出明确数值，但表明该方法在全年尺度上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL为暖通空调系统控制提供了一种结合质量-多样性进化与强化学习的新范式，通过维护多样化的专业策略代替单一标量化策略，能够在复杂、多变的热带气候条件下实现有效的监督控制。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [257] [Forward and Inverse Virtual Metrology for Phototransistor Gain: A Hierarchical, Uncertainty-Aware Approach for Small Production Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11868)
*Mahshid Amirabgir,Lorenza Ferrario,Paolo Conci,Mahdieh Amirabgir,Giancarlo Orengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究硅双极光电晶体管制造过程中，如何在少量工艺批次数据下，利用层次结构模型预测器件增益并反向搜索工艺配方，同时提供多层级数据质量评估工具。研究发现约一半增益方差来自批次间差异，并据此构建了考虑不确定性的前向预测器与逆向搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅双极光电晶体管的工艺流定制、优化和稳定化需要数月洁净室时间才能在器件测量前完成，因此需要一种能够从工艺参数预测器件增益的模型。传统虚拟计量学依赖于大规模数据语料库，而实际制造历史仅包含十三到十四个工艺批次，属于小样本、层级结构化的场景，现有方法不适用。

Method: 1) 对器件增益进行方差分解，区分批次间与批次内方差；2) 构建前向增益预测器，输出相对信号并带有不确定性估计；3) 设计逆向搜索算法，给定目标增益返回对应工艺配方；4) 提出针对制造嵌套物理实体（批次、晶圆、芯片）的多层级数据质量评估方法，包含显式跨层级关联分数。

Result: 方差分解显示器件增益约一半方差存在于工艺批次之间而非批次内部，说明仅依赖配方进行预测存在固有上限。所提出的前向预测器提供相对且含不确定性信息的信号，逆向搜索可为目标增益找到工艺配方，数据质量评估框架适用于小样本层级制造数据。数据集和分析代码已公开用于完整复现。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在小样本层级结构化的制造场景中，即使预测精度受批次间方差固有约束，仍可通过层次建模、不确定性量化和多层级数据质量评估提供有价值的工艺指导。该方法的贡献在于其价值与预测精度不成比例——在数月洁净室时间的机会成本下，即便粗略的预测也具有实际意义。

Abstract: The customization, optimization and stabilization of the process flow of a silicon bipolar phototransistor commits months of cleanroom time before a finished device can be measured, so a model that predicts device gain from process parameters before a run has value out of proportion to its accuracy. We study this problem on a real fabrication history, thirteen to fourteen process runs of a single device: a small-sample, hierarchically structured setting unlike the large-corpus regime of conventional virtual metrology. Decomposing the variance of device gain, we find that roughly half of it lies between process runs rather than within them, so recipe-only prediction is bounded by construction. Building on these findings we provide a forward gain predictor with a relative, uncertainty-aware signal, an inverse search that returns recipes for a target gain, and, as the foundation for all of it, a multi-level data-quality assessment tailored to the nested physical entities of fabrication (batch, wafer, die) with an explicit cross-level linkage score. The normalized dataset and analysis code are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [258] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，用于预测完整财务报表未来1-20个季度的78个报表行项目。Forma是一种将报表视为(账户, 季度, 值)元组集合的Transformer，通过最大化掩码元组高斯似然进行训练，在所有对比基线（经典机器学习、梯度提升、时序基础模型、大语言模型）中表现最佳，且预测优势随预测期限延长而扩大，其预测区间具有良好的覆盖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贴现现金流估值中大部分企业价值位于一年以上的预测窗口，但现有工作未能在更长时间范围内联合预测完整财务报表。作者认为专业化训练（针对财务报表领域）比泛化的大规模模型更为有效，因此构建可复现的基准来推动该方向的研究。

Method: 构建ProForma-20Q基准：包含匿名化企业过去78个报表行项目的1-20季度预测任务，以变化空间R²作为评分指标。提出Forma模型：一种Transformer架构，将报表读取为(账户, 季度, 值)元组集合，通过最大化掩码元组高斯似然进行训练，并可输出高斯预测区间。还探索了通过投影使预测满足会计恒等式的方法以及支持场景分析的元组接口。

Result: Forma在所有对比方法中表现最优，包括经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型和前沿大语言模型。其优势随预测时域延长而扩大（恰是估值最需要的区域），高斯预测区间从未出现覆盖不足。预测几乎满足会计恒等式，且通过后处理恢复精确一致性不会带来统计显著的精度损失。固定未来收入路径可提升其余报表项目的预测精度。

Conclusion: 专业化训练（针对财务报表的定制模型）在财务预测任务上优于通用大规模模型。Forma的元组接口设计支持无需重训的场景分析，为财务报表预测提供了一种高效、可靠且可解释的解决方案，并通过公开基准ProForma-20Q促进该领域可复现研究。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [259] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重重量的训练自由解码时方法来近似监督微调（SFT）的分布效应。WFT通过计算训练序列上的监督残差，并利用dropout诱导的交叉协方差估计跨前缀传输算子，将残差传输到当前提示词的logit空间进行修正，从而在个性化场景中避免每个作者的独立权重更新、存储和重训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）是适应LLM到目标分布的標準方法，但在个性化等场景中，每个作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练，成本过高。研究者希望找到一种无需权重更新的方法，在解码时近似SFT的分布效应，以大幅降低计算和存储开销。

Method: WFT在推理时计算作者训练序列上的监督残差，通过从dropout诱导的交叉协方差估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传输到当前提示词的logit空间。该算子捕捉一个上下文中的扰动如何传播到另一个上下文的预测，用logit空间修正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得最佳平均性能，在单个任务上匹配或超过SFT，并在平均性能上优于其他轻量级基线。在预算受控比较中，WFT使用不到SFT的7%有效计算量即可接近其性能。logit级分析显示WFT和SFT诱导的logit偏移在95%的下一个token概率质量上余弦相似度为0.875。

Conclusion: WFT通过训练自由的解码时方法有效地捕捉了监督适应的分布效应，在不修改模型权重的情况下接近SFT的性能，显著降低了计算成本，为个性化等资源受限场景提供了实用替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [260] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业场景——市政水处理厂，用于离线强化学习超参数选择。研究评估了多种校准模型方法（包括基于拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型），在高维、非平稳的传感器数据上执行nexting预测任务，结果表明这些模型能够生成逼真的长时域rollout并恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势，为离线动力学模型支持RL真实部署提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统中部署的关键障碍是超参数选择，特别是在缺乏模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。现有校准模型仅在简单模拟环境中被评估，缺乏真实工业场景的验证，因此本文希望在真实世界环境中验证校准模型的有效性。

Method: 本文在真实市政水处理厂部署校准模型，评估多种方法（包括使用拉普拉斯距离度量的k-近邻模型），处理高维、非平稳的传感器数据。同时研究了校准模型在年尺度数据集上的扩展性、对预训练智能体微调学习率选择的支持能力，以及其在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果证明校准模型能够生成逼真的长时域rollout，并能恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势，为离线动力学模型在真实环境RL部署中提供了概念验证，同时也揭示了实际应用中需要关注的工程挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究为使用离线动力学模型支持强化学习在真实环境中的部署提供了概念验证，但同时也指出了重要的实际挑战，为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

</details>


### [261] [Market-Information-Aware Gated-LoRA of Foundation Models for Transferable Day-Ahead Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11359)
*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向日前电价预测的市场信息感知自适应框架，通过将Chronos-2时序基础模型与多源市场信息（MSMI）接口及门控低秩适配器（LoRA）相结合，实现跨市场迁移。在四个中国省级现货市场的留一市场测试中，该框架相比零样本Chronos-2在MAE/RMSE上分别降低6.24%/7.99%，相比普通Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%，为数据稀缺的电价市场提供了一条实用迁移路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但现有监督方法高度依赖特定市场的历史数据，在新建立或数据稀缺的市场中效果受限。本文旨在解决跨市场电价预测中的数据稀缺问题，探索如何利用基础模型的迁移能力实现数据匮乏场景下的有效预测。

Method: 提出的框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7日价格上下文与预清算供需、备用、维护、发电容量和联络线变量对齐；(2) 源域门控低秩适配器（LoRA），仅更新约1%的模型参数且不需要目标市场标签，门控机制根据备用紧张度和运行状态信号对冻结的源适配器进行缩放。评估采用留一市场协议检验跨市场迁移性。

Result: 在四个中国省级日前现货市场的实验中，所提框架相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2将平均MAE/RMSE分别降低6.24%/7.99%，相比普通Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验表明，该增益不能由学习到的全局标量或随机门初始化复现，但相比Source-LoRA的额外改进有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入与状态依赖的门控LoRA组合为数据稀缺的电力市场提供了实用的迁移路径，验证了将时序基础模型与领域知识适配相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [262] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了大语言模型的分词器词表大小并非一个固定常数，而是服务场景的函数。通过形式化全生命周期部署成本，并在两类GPU（A10G和A100）上进行受控实验，发现推理最优词表随批处理大小从32k（B=1）变化到524k（B≥64），差异达16倍。模型质量（BPB）在最优词表区间内近似不变（<2%差异），因此词表选择可视为纯系统工程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词器词表大小是大语言模型基础设施的基础设计选择，但传统上在训练阶段凭惯例固定，而非基于实际部署分析。作者指出成本最优词表并非恒定值，而是随服务场景（推理量、批处理大小）变化，当前实践缺乏系统化的选择指导。

Method: 作者形式化总部署成本为C_lifecycle(V) = C_train(V) + λ·C_infer(V, B)，其中λ为推理量、B为服务批大小。在两种GPU（A10G，ridge≈117 FLOP/byte；A100，ridge≈183 FLOP/byte）上，在1.3-2.3B模型规模下，分别评估训练成本、推理成本（含V×d unembedding矩阵读取的摊销效应）和质量（BPB）三个维度，进而推导出不同部署场景下的词表大小最优解。

Result: (1) 推理最优词表大小随批处理规模发生16倍偏移：B=1时为32k，B≥64时为524k，这是由V×d unembedding矩阵读取的摊销驱动的；(2) 质量（BPB）在V=65k时最优，确认了词表偏好依赖于模型规模；(3) 全生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署中最多相差16倍。质量在最优范围内近似不变（<2% BPB差异）。

Conclusion: 词表大小在最优范围内是纯系统工程优化，不会带来质量损失。实践指导：端侧部署（B=1）应使用V≈32k；数据中心服务（B≥64，λ≥10）应使用V≈131-262k。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [263] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）方法，识别出RLVR中leave-one-out组相对得分梯度的统计本质是二阶U统计量（成对梯度项），而非独立点贡献之和。PAIR将短rollout前缀视为顶点、配对梯度项视为边构建对比图，通过前缀预测器估计正确性与剩余token成本，用凸优化在期望后缀token预算下选择正向延续概率，并对每条边按其联合包含概率的逆进行加权，从而实现对完整候选对梯度的设计无偏估计。在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上，PAIR相比最强点式分配器平均准确率提升+1.2/+1.4，同时相比全组GRPO节省51%/52%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR的预算分配器（如按难度为prompt、rollout或token分配预算）本质上基于"点式"（pointwise）的难度或效用概念。但作者发现了一个统计错配：未裁剪的留一法组相对得分梯度并非独立点贡献之和，而是一个关于rollout对的二阶U统计量。这意味着完成一个rollout会与所有其他已完成的rollout产生对比信息，自适应端点选择会改变可见的成对项。这揭示出现有分配器在统计基础上存在根本缺陷，需要新的方法予以校正。

Method: PAIR方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 将短rollout前缀视为顶点、pair-gradient项视为边，构建对比图（contrast graph）框架；(2) 使用前缀预测器（prefix-only predictor）估计正确性和剩余token成本，通过凸优化（convex design）在期望后缀token预算约束下选择正向继续概率；(3) 对每条由已完整顶点诱导出的边，以其记录的联合包含概率（joint inclusion probability）的倒数进行逆加权（inverse-weighting），从而在条件独立同策略rollout和无裁剪、无标准化目标下，实现对完整候选对梯度的设计无偏（design-unbiased）估计。

Result: 在计算量匹配的RLVR运行中（Qwen3-1.7B和4B），PAIR相比最强的点式分配器分别提升平均准确率+1.2和+1.4个百分点，同时相比全组GRPO节省51%和52%的生成token。冻结样本总体的估计审计（frozen-population estimator audit）确认：未加权自适应选择是有偏的，而pair-inclusion校正能在匹配的后缀成本下恢复完整对的估计目标。

Conclusion: RLVR的预算分配本质上应基于成对（pairwise）结构而非点式结构；通过将自适应选择过程的包含概率显式建模并逆加权，可以在显著降低计算成本的同时提高训练效果，且估计保持设计无偏性。这为未来RLVR的效率优化提供了更严格的统计基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>


### [264] [Towards an approach to multivariate outlier detection for District Heating System data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11375)
*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对区域供热系统某变电站传输热能耗数据，结合室外环境温度，对比多种多变量异常检测方法（Z-score、马氏距离、PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling T²检验），旨在发现异常运行工况，最终采用三种有效方法（PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling）的集成投票策略作为最终方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在识别区域供热系统（DHS）中的异常运行状况，以发现降低天然气消耗和CO2排放的机会，推动供热系统节能降碳。

Method: 对比多种异常检测方法：Z-score（单变量基准）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、孤立森林（Isolation Forest）和Hotelling T²检验；考虑室外环境温度作为额外变量；并针对领域特殊性（如零传输能量时点不代表离网）进行了专门的考虑；最终采用基于三种有效方法检测结果一致性的集成方法。

Result: 实验表明PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling T²检验提供了相关且有效的异常检测结果，而Z-score（单变量）和马氏距离效果不佳；最终采用三种有效方法达成共识的集成策略作为最终方案。
