Abstract: Modern stochastic predictors can model rich, multi-modal outcome distributions. However, this expressive power comes with challenges in ensuring robust predictions $-$ a critical requirement in safety-critical domains. Randomized smoothing is a leading technique for improving robustness, particularly against adversarial perturbations. Yet, in stochastic multi-modal regression settings, randomized smoothing often fails due to mode collapse, yielding averaged predictions that do not reflect the underlying distribution. To address this limitation, we propose clustered $α$-smoothing, a framework that (1) partitions noisy samples using an arbitrary clustering algorithm, (2) applies $α$-smoothing locally within each cluster, and (3) combines the resulting predictions into a mixture distribution. By interpreting the smoothing distribution as a mixture of $α$-smoothers, we derive a lower bound on the probability that the smoothed prediction lies within a union of compact regions corresponding to distinct modes. We empirically evaluate our framework on two benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods. In stochastic trajectory prediction on a driving simulator dataset, our approach achieves, on average, a $27\%$ lower Wasserstein distance to the ground-truth distribution compared to $α$-smoothing. In quadrotor control, where modes correspond to distinct feasible paths to a target, our method reduces the collision rate by $81\%$ relative to the state-of-the-art randomized smoothing.

</details>


### [332] [Towards Truly Unsupervised Evaluation of Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12057)
*Hafiz Saud Arshad,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了现有的"无监督"特征选择评估方法，指出它们本质上仍依赖于标签信息（至多是"无监督下游任务下的监督评估"），并提出了一个真正无监督的评估框架，利用主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）在完全无标签信息的情况下衡量特征选择算法的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督特征选择评估方法存在严重的设计缺陷，其"无监督"性质存疑——它们在评估过程中实际上隐式或显式地使用了标签信息，违背了无监督评估的初衷。因此需要一种真正不依赖任何标签信息的评估框架来公正衡量特征选择算法的质量。

Method: 提出一个真正无监督的评估框架，该框架结合了无监督主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）技术，在不使用任何标签信息的前提下度量特征选择方法的质量。核心思想是通过PCA捕捉数据结构，利用最优传输度量原始特征空间与选择后特征空间之间的分布差异。

Result: 论文通过理论分析和实证论证揭示了现有无监督评估方法的缺陷，并展示了所提出的PCA与最优传输相结合的评估框架能够在完全无监督的条件下有效衡量特征选择算法的质量。

Conclusion: 现有的所谓"无监督"特征选择评估方法实际上是有缺陷的，至多只能算作在无监督下游任务下的监督评估。本文提出的基于PCA和最优传输的评估框架为实现真正无监督的特征选择质量评估提供了可行的新路径。

Abstract: Feature selection is one of the most important and fundamental tasks in data mining, tackled by a family of methods with an established set of evaluation techniques to measure the quality of a specific method. Most of the methods commonly used for the unsupervised evaluation of feature selection algorithms suffer from critical design flaws which question their unsupervised nature. In this paper, we provide a critical discussion on the established allegedly unsupervised evaluation techniques, and shed light on the reasons why they are not truly unsupervised but, at best, supervised evaluation under an unsupervised downstream task. We also propose a novel, truly unsupervised evaluation framework to measure the quality of the feature selection algorithms without any form of information about the labels. The proposed framework utilizes unsupervised Principal Component Analysis, and optimal transport to measure the quality of the feature selection methods in a truly unsupervised manner.

</details>


### [333] [Faithful, Sufficient and Understandable: Rethinking Graph Counterfactual Explanations via Discrete Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12083)
*David Bechtoldt,Sidney Bender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GDCE-I，一种基于离散去噪扩散模型和图反转方案的图反事实解释方法，在保持数据流形的同时探索完整编辑空间，并在统一评估框架下大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在各领域表现优异但缺乏内在可解释性，限制了其在高风险场景中的应用。图反事实解释旨在找到改变模型预测的最小结构修改，但搜索空间离散且组合爆炸，且需满足类别节点/边类型和化学价等域规则。现有方法要么编辑偏离数据流形，要么搜索未覆盖完整编辑空间，没有方法能同时兼顾两者。

Method: GDCE-I采用离散去噪扩散模型作为生成先验，配合一种新颖的离散反转（inversion）方案，使编辑既落在数据流形内（分布感知），又能够遍及完整域编辑空间。此外，论文推导了一套反事实解释的期望指标（desiderata）框架，并在统一协议下对所有方法进行评估。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，GDCE-I在所定义的评价框架上大幅优于相关工作。在分子领域，定性结果进一步表明GDCE-I能够生成可解释且落在分布内的解决方案。

Conclusion: GDCE-I解决了图反事实解释中"数据流形保持"与"编辑空间覆盖"不可兼得的问题，并通过系统化的评估框架推动了图反事实解释的标准化评价。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on graph-structured data across domains such as chemistry, biology, and network analysis, yet they provide no intrinsic explanation of their predictions. This limits their adoption in high-stakes and safety-critical settings. Counterfactual explanations address this by revealing the minimal structural modifications that would change a model's prediction. On graphs, however, such a modification is hard to produce. The search space is discrete and combinatorial, and a valid answer must respect categorical node and edge types together with domain rules such as chemical valency in the case of molecular graphs. Existing explainers give up one of two things. Either edits are not held on the data manifold, or the search does not span the full edit space. We propose Graph Diffusion Counterfactual Explanation via Inversion (GDCE-I), which gives up neither. A discrete denoising diffusion model with a novel discrete inversion scheme enables distribution-aware edits leveraging the whole domain edit space. We further address the incomplete and inconsistent evaluation of graph counterfactuals by deriving a framework of explanation desiderata and applying it to every method under one shared protocol. Across four benchmarks, GDCE-I outperforms related work by a large margin on the defined framework. For the molecular domain, we further qualitatively show that GDCE-I attains interpretable in-distribution solutions.

</details>


### [334] [NAE: Normalizing AutoEncoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12084)
*Muhammad Abdur Rafae,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对具有近似逆的归一化流（称为流自编码器）进行了理论分析，证明了现有方法所提损失是次优的，并提出Normalizing Autoencoder（NAE）模型，通过新颖的条件损失对齐代理损失梯度与重建损失梯度，在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流与近似逆结合的范式（涵盖全维度和瓶颈设置）已在多个领域应用，但现有方法的训练损失被证明是次优的——编码器和解码器代理必须与重建损失对齐优化，此前缺乏对这一问题的理论认识。

Method: 作者将全维度和瓶颈设置的归一化流统一归为"流自编码器"（flow autoencoders），并从理论上分析其训练动态，证明现有损失函数次优性。基于此发现，提出NAE模型，其核心是采用一种新颖的条件损失，使代理损失梯度与重建损失梯度对齐。

Result: 在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准上的大量实验表明，NAE在所有基准上均达到最先进（SOTA）性能，验证了损失对齐策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 这项工作强调了损失对齐在流自编码器中的重要性，确立了NAE作为一种强大的生成框架，并为该领域的损失设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We consider the setting of Normalizing flows with approximate inverses, an established paradigm spanning both full-dimensional ($d=D$) and bottleneck ($d<D$) settings, and group these models under the term flow autoencoders. We present a theoretical investigation into their training dynamics and prove that the proposed loss used by existing approaches is suboptimal; specifically, both encoder and decoder surrogates must be optimized in alignment with reconstruction loss. Guided by these insights, we propose Normalizing Autoencoder (NAE), which employs a novel conditional loss that aligns the surrogate loss gradient with that of reconstruction loss, directly improving upon the current standard. Extensive experiments across molecule generation, tabular data, and image benchmarks demonstrate that NAE achieves state of the art performance. Our work highlights the importance of loss alignment in flow autoencoders and establishes NAE as a powerful generative framework.

</details>


### [335] [Task- and dataset-specific information in protein language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12090)
*Roman Joeres,Ilya Senatorov,Olga V. Kalinina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了13个蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）在11个数据集的15个下游任务中的层间嵌入信息量，发现最后一层嵌入并不总是最优的。研究揭示了任务类型与信息在PLM层间分布的关联：残基级任务的信息随层数递增，而全蛋白任务的性能更多由数据集决定（DMS数据适合浅层嵌入，自然蛋白多样数据集适合深层嵌入），且PLMs在人工蛋白上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）在计算生物学中广泛应用，但社区普遍存在一个共识性做法：直接使用模型最后一层的嵌入进行下游任务。然而，模型内部各层嵌入所包含的信息是否等价、最后一层是否真的是最优选择，这些问题尚未被系统研究。作者旨在揭开PLM内部行为的黑箱，为下游任务提供更合理的嵌入选择指导。

Method: 作者选取13个PLMs，覆盖11个数据集上的15个下游任务（DTs）。对每个模型中每一层的嵌入分别训练探测模型（probe models）进行分类或回归，比较各层嵌入在下游任务上的性能差异。同时计算各层潜空间的特征（如维度、聚类结构等）以估计潜在信息量，并分析任务类型、预训练目标、数据集来源与信息在层间分布之间的关联规律。

Result: （1）多数PLMs中，最后一层嵌入并非下游任务的最优选择；（2）残基级任务的信息量随网络层数增加而稳步提升，这与预训练目标（如掩码语言建模预测单个残基）的相似性有关；（3）全蛋白级任务的性能主要由数据集特性决定而非任务本身：含深度突变扫描（DMS）数据的数据集在浅层嵌入上表现更好，而含多样化天然蛋白的数据集则更受益于深层嵌入；（4）PLMs在人工（合成）蛋白上的下游任务性能显著下降。

Conclusion: PLM的中间层嵌入包含有价值的信息，最后一层并非普适最优选择。下游任务的最佳嵌入层选择应根据任务类型和数据特性进行定制：残基级任务倾向使用深层嵌入，DMS类数据集适合浅层嵌入，多样化天然蛋白数据集适合深层嵌入。此外，PLMs对人工蛋白的泛化能力存在明显短板，这提醒研究者在人工蛋白相关应用中需谨慎评估PLM的适用性。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have transferred the latest advances from natural language processing to computational biology. These models, trained on large corpora of protein sequence data, are widely used to translate amino acid sequences into latent-space embeddings, ready for use in diverse downstream tasks (DTs). By a common consensus, embeddings from the model's last layer are used, and the model's internal behavior remains poorly understood. We analyzed 13 PLMs across 15 DTs from 11 datasets to investigate the informativeness of embeddings created in intermediate PLM layers. We trained probe models on embeddings from each layer, compared their performance, and computed characteristics of the latent spaces they span to estimate the information they contain, and found that the last layers of PLMs rarely contained embeddings that led to the best results on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we identified a connection between DTs and the distribution across PLMs' layers of the relevant information to predict that task. For example, similarity between the pre-training objective and the objective of predicting properties of individual residues leads to a steady increase in understanding of such tasks across the layers of PLMs. On the other hand, for whole-protein tasks, we observe that the dataset, rather than the task itself, defines PLMs' ability to perform well on a DT. Embeddings from shallow layers of PLMs perform better for datasets that contain deep mutational scan (DMS) data, while datasets containing diverse natural proteins find most useful embeddings in the models' deeper layers. Additionally, we discover that the performance of PLMs drops significantly when tasks are introduced for artificial proteins.

</details>


### [336] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文围绕深度学习模型在高风险应用中的置信度校准问题展开，针对标签噪声、无监督域适应和隐私保护三种现实场景，提出了噪声感知的校准框架、无目标标签的校准方法以及局部差分隐私的共形预测框架，实现了可靠性、隐私性和噪声鲁棒性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法通常假设存在干净的验证数据，这在实践中因标签噪声和领域偏移往往不成立。在高风险应用中，可靠的置信度估计与预测本身同等重要，因此需要开发在非理想数据条件下依然有效的校准方法。

Method: （1）针对标签噪声，提出基于估计噪声模型的框架，建模噪声标签与干净标签分布间的关系以重构无噪声置信度估计，并扩展至共形预测（CP），通过估计干净的一致性分数实现可靠的集合预测；（2）针对无监督域适应，利用源域性能和域间差异估计目标域精度，实现在无目标标签条件下的校准；（3）针对隐私保护场景，提出局部差分隐私的共形预测框架，在保证隐私的同时兼顾计算可行性和预测可靠性。

Result: 该研究在标签噪声、无监督域适应和隐私保护三种不理想场景下均实现了有效的置信度校准，扩展了共形预测方法的适用范围，使得不确定性量化在噪声、域偏移和隐私约束下依然可靠。

Conclusion: 本文架起了校准理论与安全关键场景实际部署之间的桥梁，为可靠、隐私保护和噪声鲁棒的神经网络预测提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [337] [Beyond Parameter Space: NTK-Guided Personalized Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12108)
*Mirko Konstantin,Stefan Zachow,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGHTYEAR是一个联邦学习框架，通过NTK（神经正切核）在函数空间中评估客户端更新的预测行为相似性，利用P2P拓扑让每个客户端在私有验证数据上评估并选择性聚合对其目标域有益的更新，解决参数空间相似度在异构非IID数据下作为更新选择准则的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，现有方法在参数空间中通过比较模型参数或梯度来确定哪些客户端更新有利于聚合。然而在异构非IID数据下，参数空间相似度无法很好地反映预测行为，导致与目标域不一致的更新（如异构数据或故障客户端产生的）会降低本地模型性能。

Method: LIGHTYEAR提出在函数空间中执行更新选择：利用NTK（神经正切核）一致性分数刻画预测行为，为每个客户端确定个性化聚合集合；同时采用P2P拓扑，客户端直接交换更新并在私有验证数据上评估传入模型，通过正则化聚合规则提高异构环境下的稳定性。

Result: 在五个数据集和九个基线方法上，LIGHTYEAR持续优于集中式FL基线和现有的P2P方法。

Conclusion: 函数空间中的更新选择比参数空间相似度更能有效识别对目标域有益的客户端更新，结合P2P拓扑和正则化聚合规则，LIGHTYEAR在异构联邦学习环境中显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients while keeping data local. A central challenge is determining which client updates are beneficial for aggregation with respect to each client's target domain. Existing methods typically address this problem in parameter space by comparing model parameters or gradients. However, parameter-space similarity can be a poor proxy for predictive behavior, especially under heterogeneous, non-IID data. Consequently, updates that are misaligned with a client's target domain, including those caused by heterogeneous data or malfunctioning clients, may degrade local model performance.
  We propose Local Inference Guided Aggregation for Heterogeneous Training Environments to Yield Enhancement Through Agreement and Regularization (LIGHTYEAR), a federated learning framework that performs update selection in function space. LIGHTYEAR uses an NTK-based agreement score to characterize predictive behavior and determine a personalized aggregation set for each client. By relating model parameters to local predictive responses, the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) provides a more expressive criterion for update selection than parameter-space similarity alone.
  Because function-space information is not available before aggregation in conventional centralized FL, LIGHTYEAR uses a peer-to-peer (P2P) topology in which clients exchange updates directly and evaluate incoming models on private validation data. Each client selects only updates that are beneficial for its own target domain and aggregates them using a regularized rule that improves stability under heterogeneity.
  Across five datasets and nine baseline methods, LIGHTYEAR consistently outperforms centralized FL baselines and existing P2P approaches.

</details>


### [338] [Attractor Image-Based Deep Learning of Arterial Pulse Waves for Age Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12117)
*Sara Vardanega,Patrick Segers,Philip Aston,Ernst Rietzschel,Jordi Alastruey,Manasi Nandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用对称投影吸引子重建（SPAR）方法将光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和动脉张力测量信号转化为图像，训练卷积神经网络（CNN）对健康成年人进行紧密间隔年龄分组（35-40岁与50-55岁）分类，在内部和外部测试集上均取得F1分数超过70%的一致性表现，证明SPAR衍生的脉搏波图像包含具有判别力的形态学特征，为智能可穿戴设备早期风险检测奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动脉脉搏波形态随年龄变化可反映心血管系统的结构与功能改变，因此血管年龄是心血管健康的重要替代标志物，过早血管老化预示疾病风险增加。脉搏波分析有望为无症状成人的风险分层提供支持，但目前缺乏能够从脉搏波信号中提取足够判别性特征以区分相近年龄健康人群的方法。

Method: 采用SPAR方法将PPG和动脉张力测量的脉搏波时间序列数据转换为图像，利用转换后的SPAR图像训练CNN分类器，将健康受试者分为两个年龄间隔较小的组别（35-40岁和50-55岁），并在内部和外部测试集上评估模型性能。

Result: 模型在内部和外部测试集上均表现出一致的分类性能，对PPG和张力测量信号均取得超过70%的F1分数，证明SPAR图像能够捕获健康成年人中年龄相近群体间具有判别性的脉搏波形态特征。

Conclusion: 这项概念验证研究表明SPAR方法在脉搏波图像化表征方面具有潜力，为未来利用智能可穿戴设备进行早期心血管风险检测的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Arterial pulse waveform morphology evolves with age, reflecting structural and functional changes in the cardiovascular system. Thus, vascular age is a valuable surrogate marker of cardiovascular health, and premature vascular ageing can indicate increased disease risk. Pulse wave analysis could support risk stratification in otherwise asymptomatic adults. We transformed pulse wave time-series data from photoplethysmography (PPG) and arterial tonometry into images, using the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method. These SPAR images were used to train a convolutional neural network to classify healthy subjects into two closely spaced age groups (35-40 and 50-55 years). The model demonstrated consistent classification performance across internal and external test sets, achieving F1 scores above 70% for both PPG and tonometry signals. These results suggest that SPAR-derived pulse wave images contain discriminative morphological features even among healthy adults close in age. This proof-of-concept lays the groundwork for future research into the use of SPAR for early risk detection using smart wearables.

</details>


### [339] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一般拟阵约束下的非负子模最大化问题，允许离线算法访问任意受控值预言机。核心结果是针对"Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process"（SGS-Poisson）算法的对抗鲁棒性定理：在不修改泊松强度、单元素交换规则或恶意丢弃步骤的前提下，该算法对非单调目标保持1/e的极限逼近因子，对单调目标保持1-1/e的逼近因子。在受控预言机误差ξ下，算法分别实现(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)和(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)的期望值下界，并仅需Õ(nk²ε⁻²)次预言机调用。由此引申的离线到在线归约可产生全bandit CMAB算法，用于一般拟阵约束的子模奖励，具有精确的1/e和1-1/e极限逼近-遗憾因子，遗憾为Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模最大化在组合优化、机器学习等领域有广泛应用，而拟阵约束下的子模最大化是核心问题。实际场景中算法往往无法获得精确的函数值，只能访问带有噪声或误差的"受控预言机"。此外，将离线算法推广到在线（bandit）设定需要算法具备对抗鲁棒性，即对预言机误差的稳定性。本文旨在证明SGS-Poisson算法在受控预言机下的鲁棒性，并据此构建高效的组合多臂赌博机（CMAB）算法。

Method: 本文提出并证明了一个对抗鲁棒性定理：针对SGS-Poisson算法（包含泊松过程驱动的贪婪交换机制和恶意丢弃步骤），证明其在任意受控预言机下不经修改即可保持原有逼近因子。核心技术包括：(1) 利用SGS-Poisson的泊松强度不变性来抵御预言机扰动；(2) 通过误差传播分析将预言机误差ξ汇总为O(kξ)的总价值损失；(3) 基于误差容忍的离线算法建立离线到在线的归约，从而推导出全bandit CMAB算法及其遗憾上界。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) 对于非单调目标函数，SGS-Poisson在任意受控预言机下实现期望值至少(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)，逼近因子为1/e；(2) 对于单调目标函数，实现期望值至少(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)，逼近因子为1-1/e；(3) 算法复杂度为Õ(nk²ε⁻²)次预言机调用；(4) 由此得到的CMAB算法实现Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})遗憾，达到精确的极限逼近-遗憾权衡因子1/e和1-1/e。

Conclusion: 本文证明了SGS-Poisson算法对受控预言机误差具有对抗鲁棒性，即无需修改算法结构即可在噪声环境下保持最优逼近因子。这一结果填补了离线鲁棒子模优化与在线bandit学习之间的理论空白，并为一般拟阵约束下的全bandit CMAB子模奖励优化提供了首个具有精确极限因子的算法与遗憾界。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [340] [HYDRA: Hyperbolic Dynamic Representation Architecture for Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12194)
*Zhao Su,Yuxin Xia,Haoran Li,Jun Shen,Qi Zhu,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HYDRA是一种参数高效的KAN超双曲扩展方法，通过将双曲空间中的表示与低秩原型块结合，显著减少了KAN的参数冗余，同时提升了预测性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN虽然通过可学习单变量函数替代标量权重增强了非线性逼近能力，但每个连接都分配独立函数导致参数严重冗余，制约了其可扩展性和效率。

Method: HYDRA将向量值输入映射到Poincaré球的有界双曲隐空间，在切空间执行KAN风格的更新，并采用低秩原型块在隐藏维度间共享函数变换。双曲表示提供结构化径向坐标用于解释，半径控制通过防止边界饱和提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，HYDRA在保持竞争性或更优预测性能的同时，显著提升了参数效率和表示可解释性。

Conclusion: HYDRA通过双曲几何与低秩共享机制有效解决了KAN的参数冗余问题，为参数高效的函数逼近网络提供了新思路，同时增强了模型的可解释性和训练稳定性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) enhance nonlinear function approximation by replacing scalar weights with learnable univariate functions. However, assigning an independent function to every connection results in substantial parameter redundancy, limiting their scalability and efficiency. To reduce this redundancy, we introduce \textbf{HY}perbolic \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}rchitecture (HYDRA), a parameter-efficient hyperbolic extension of KAN that combines spline-based functional learning with representations in the Poincaré ball. HYDRA maps vector-valued inputs into a bounded hyperbolic latent space, performs KAN-style updates in tangent space, and employs a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions. The resulting hyperbolic representations provide a structured radial coordinate for interpretation, while radius control improves training stability by preventing boundary saturation. Extensive experiments across eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYDRA consistently achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and representation interpretability.

</details>


### [341] [ScreenShot: A Foundation Model for Few-Shot Combination Drug Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12219)
*Antoine de Mathelin,Christopher Tosh,Wesley Tansey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScreenShot是一个层次化Transformer模型，在大规模药物筛选数据上预训练，通过上下文学习直接基于功能测量预测新患者对联合用药的反应，无需微调和分子谱分析，在四个验证集上超越所有基线，并能通过主动学习策略以1/3预算实现同等命中检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合用药可降低耐药风险，但组合筛选的搜索空间巨大，导致传统实验成本高昂、耗时且常技术上不可行。现有预测模型通常需要对每个样本进行分子特征分析并逐队列训练，在时间和组织样本稀缺时难以应用。

Method: ScreenShot采用层次化Transformer架构，在覆盖3700种药物和6000个生物学样本的40个药物筛选数据集上预训练。其架构设计与筛选数据的嵌套结构（数据集-样本-药物组合）相匹配，通过上下文学习（few-shot）直接处理功能测量数据，无需微调和分子谱分析。另提出加权k-means++主动学习策略，利用模型内部表征选择实验方案。

Result: 在四个留出数据集上，ScreenShot在预测准确性和选择性有效治疗识别方面均优于所有基线。主动学习策略在仅使用1/3实验预算的情况下达到与均匀筛选相同的命中检测效果。

Conclusion: ScreenShot证明了无需分子谱分析和大规模微调，仅通过预训练加上下文学习即可实现跨数据集的药物组合反应预测，并展示了模型表征可直接服务于实验设计优化，为药物组合筛选提供了高效、经济的新范式。

Abstract: Treating patients with combinations of drugs reduces the risk of resistance to any individual drug. Finding effective combinations is difficult because the large search space makes combinatorial screens prohibitively expensive, time consuming, and often technically infeasible. Predictive models can fill this gap, yet existing methods typically require molecular profiling of each sample and per-cohort training, limiting their applicability when time and tissue are scarce. To address this challenge, we introduce ScreenShot, a hierarchical transformer pretrained on 40 drug screening datasets covering 3,700 drugs and 6,000 biological samples, whose architecture mirrors the nested structure of screening data. Given a few-shot context of observations from a new patient, ScreenShot predicts the response of the sample to combination therapies through in-context learning, operating directly on functional measurements with no fine-tuning and no molecular profiling. On four held-out datasets, ScreenShot outperforms all baselines in both prediction accuracy and identification of selectively effective treatments. ScreenShot's internal representations are directly useful for experimental design: we use them to drive a weighted k-means++ active learning strategy that selects which experiments to run, achieving the same hit detection as uniform screening with a third of the budget. Source code and interactive dashboard: https://github.com/tansey-lab/screenshot.

</details>


### [342] [An Efficient Near-Optimal Algorithm for Adversarial $m$-Set Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12231)
*Francesco Bacchiocchi,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性组合式赌博机问题（m-set动作），提出一种计算高效的算法来利用动作集的结构，在多项式时间内实现对自适应非预期对手的悔恨界限O(√(dT log(K/δ)))，与EXP3-KW的高概率悔恨界相匹配，解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性组合式赌博机中，m-set动作空间大小为K=binom(d,m)，呈指数级增长，直接枚举动作集的经典算法（如EXP3-KW）虽然能保证最优悔恨界，但可能需要指数级空间。因此需要设计一种利用底层结构、无需显式枚举动作集的计算高效算法。

Method: 提出一种计算高效算法，利用所有动作损失由同一d维物品损失向量决定的结构特性，将每个采样分布仅用d个参数表示，从而在多项式时间内运行，无需枚举大小为K的动作集。

Result: 在自适应非预期对手下，以至少1-δ的概率保证悔恨界R_T = O(√(dT log(K/δ)))，与有限动作集EXP3-KW的高概率悔恨界相匹配，同时避免了后者的指数级空间开销。

Conclusion: 该算法在保持最优高概率悔恨界的同时实现了多项式时间复杂度，成功解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。

Abstract: We study adversarial combinatorial bandits with $m$-set actions, where at each round the learner selects $m$ out of $d$ items and observes only the aggregate loss of the selected items. The resulting action set contains $K=\binom{d}{m}$ elements and can therefore be exponentially large. Nevertheless, the loss of every action is determined by the same $d$-dimensional vector of item losses. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits this structure without explicitly enumerating the action set. Against adaptive non-anticipating adversaries, it guarantees, with probability at least $1-δ$, regret against the best fixed action of \[
  R_T =
  O\left(\sqrt{dT\log(K/δ)}\right). \] This matches the high-probability regret bound of the finite-action EXP3-KW algorithm of Zimmert and Lattimore, whose direct implementation may require exponential space. Our algorithm instead represents each sampling distribution with $d$ parameters and runs in polynomial time without enumerating the action set. Thus, it resolves the open problem posed by Maiti et al.

</details>


### [343] [Calibration Bets on the Past: Post-Training Quantization for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12259)
*Junyi Ye,Ivy Gateri Wanjiku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后训练量化（PTQ）中激活校准对金融预测模型的影响，发现8比特下校准几乎无影响，而在4比特下成为预测性能的主导因素；百分位校准替代绝对最大校准可恢复53-94%的性能退化，并指出激活校准是财务预测中4比特PTQ部署的一等决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融预测模型通常在全精度下开发，但生产环境需要低精度推理以降低内存和计算成本。后训练量化（PTQ）无需重训练即可实现低精度部署，然而激活量化需要校准（从历史数据估计激活范围），这一部署决策对金融预测的重要性此前尚不明确，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在S&P 500横截面波动率预测任务上开展系统性研究：覆盖7种代表性神经网络架构、8个walk-forward测试年份（2018-2025）、共训练560个模型。对比不同激活校准策略（abs-max vs 百分位）在不同比特精度（8位vs 4位）下的量化效果，评估维度包括信息系数（IC）等预测性能指标。

Result: 发现：1) 8比特下激活校准影响甚微；2) 4比特下激活校准成为预测性能的主要决定因素；3) 默认abs-max校准下，4比特静态量化（权重+激活）导致受影响架构的全精度平均信息系数损失11-62%；4) 改用百分位校准可在受影响最严重的四个架构中恢复53-94%的性能退化；5) 最优激活范围因市场时期而异，窄范围在典型市场条件下更好，但当测试期市场离散度超过校准历史时优势减弱。

Conclusion: 激活校准是金融预测中4比特后训练量化可靠部署的一等决策变量。当性能退化严重时，8比特激活或仅权重量化的4比特方案是更稳健的部署选择。

Abstract: Financial forecasting models are typically developed in full precision, yet production deployment often requires low-precision inference to reduce memory and computational cost. Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables such deployment without retraining. However, reliable activation quantization requires calibration: activation ranges are estimated from historical data before deployment and then remain fixed during future inference. The importance of this deployment choice for financial forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of activation calibration for PTQ in cross-sectional volatility forecasting on the S&P 500. Our evaluation covers seven representative neural architectures, eight walk-forward test years (2018-2025), and 560 trained models. We find that activation calibration has little effect at 8 bits but becomes the primary determinant of predictive performance at 4 bits. Under default absolute-maximum (abs-max) calibration, static 4-bit quantization of both weights and activations removes 11-62% of the full-precision mean information coefficient in affected architectures. Replacing abs-max with percentile calibration recovers 53-94% of this degradation in the four most affected architectures. The preferred activation range also varies across market periods. Narrow ranges improve resolution under typical market conditions but lose part of their advantage when test-period market dispersion exceeds the calibration history. These findings show that activation calibration is a first-class deployment decision for reliable 4-bit PTQ in financial forecasting. When substantial degradation remains, 8-bit activations or weight-only 4-bit quantization provide more robust deployment choices.

</details>


### [344] [Earth observation embeddings are effective sub-grid descriptors for probabilistic weather downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12271)
*Pedro Sousa,Will Tebbutt,Sadiq Jaffer,Robin Young,Anil Madhavapeddy,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明长期地球观测基础模型嵌入（TESSERA）可压缩为局部地表描述符，用于概率性天气降尺度，在ERA5再分析和Aurora预报作为粗网格输入时均显著提升2m温度和10m风速的CRPS技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球天气再分析和预报在粗网格上解析大气状态，但站点级应用需要任意位置的预测，而近地表条件取决于未解析的地形和地表特性。现有概率降尺度方法依赖手工设计的地形描述符，缺乏可迁移的亚网格地表表示。

Method: 将卷积条件神经过程（convolutional conditional neural process）与学习的局部地表描述符结合：从10m分辨率的TESSERA嵌入中压缩得到地表描述符，与~25km分辨率的ERA5粗网格场一同输入降尺度模型。在空间和时间上均留出的站点上进行评估。

Result: 在五个气候多样区域中，TESSERA嵌入使2m温度CRPS技能提升11.5%，10m风速提升6.2%。地形对温度的亚网格结构解释贡献更大，而TESSERA为风速提供额外的地表信息。当粗输入从ERA5换为Aurora AI预报、以及预测无区域历史的新部署站点时，改进仍然持续。

Conclusion: 这是首次证明长期时间尺度的地球观测嵌入能够支持短期天气降尺度，其中亚网格偏差由持久地表属性系统性结构化。TESSERA地面描述符提供了可迁移的、跨时空泛化的地表表示能力。

Abstract: Global weather reanalyses and forecasts resolve the evolving atmospheric state on coarse grids, but site-specific applications require predictions at arbitrary locations where near-surface conditions also depend on unresolved terrain and land-surface properties. Existing probabilistic downscalers address this gap using hand-crafted topographic descriptors. We ask instead whether Earth observation foundation models can provide transferable sub-grid surface representations for probabilistic weather downscaling.
  We augment a convolutional conditional neural process that downscales coarse ERA5 reanalysis fields at ~25 km resolution with a learned local surface descriptor, obtained by compressing a patch of TESSERA embeddings at 10 m resolution. Although these embeddings summarise surface conditions over annual timescales, they improve downscaling of instantaneous 2 m temperature and 10 m wind speed by encoding persistent surface properties that capture a location's departure from the coarse-grid atmospheric state. Across five climatically diverse regions, the embedding improves point and probabilistic skill at stations held out in both space and time, overall improving CRPS skill by 11.5% for 2 m temperature and 6.2% for 10 m wind speed. We further analyse how its contribution differs by variable, finding that topography explains more of temperature's sub-grid structure, while TESSERA provides additional surface information for wind speed.
  These improvements persist when the coarse input is changed from ERA5 to forecasts from the Aurora AI forecasting model, and when predicting at newly deployed stations with no regional history. To our knowledge, this is the first evidence that long-timescale Earth-observation embeddings can support short-timescale weather downscaling where sub-grid departures are systematically structured by persistent surface properties.

</details>


### [345] [A Framework for Designing Reward Functions: From Objectives to Features to Human-Aligned Reward Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12302)
*Di Yang Shi,W. Bradley Knox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化流程，使非专家能够为强化学习任务实例化并迭代地设计符合人类偏好的线性奖励函数。该流程分为三步：将任务目标分解为可测量的结果变量、通过最小费用部分覆盖问题在因果DAG上选择奖励项、以及通过偏好询问将权重拟合建模为凸可行性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励函数设计是强化学习中的核心挑战，而现有方法往往需要专家知识或繁琐的人工迭代。作者目标是让非专家也能构建与给定轨迹偏好排序相一致（即人类对齐）的奖励函数，从而降低奖励设计的门槛。

Method: 该方法包含三个贡献：(1) 一套引导式工作流，将自然语言描述的任务目标分解为基本目标并推导可测量的结果变量；(2) 将奖励项选择问题归结为因果DAG上的最小费用部分覆盖问题，通过最大流在多项式时间内求解；(3) 将权重拟合建模为凸可行性问题，通过分离预言机（separation oracle）迭代收敛，以O(n log κ)次偏好查询将可行的权重区域收窄至期望容差。

Result: 该方法在理论上保证了确定性无冲突的可行权重区域，并通过分离预言机以O(n log κ)次偏好查询收窄至指定容差。据作者所知，这是首个在奖励设计过程中维持确定性无冲突可行权重区间的方案。

Conclusion: 该形式化流程为面向非专家的奖励设计提供了系统化的技术支撑，将奖励函数构建分解为可验证、有复杂度保证的步骤，在理论层面保证了偏好一致性与可收敛性。

Abstract: We present a formal process to enable non-experts to instantiate and iterate on human-aligned reward functions, i.e. reward functions that adhere to a given preference ordering over trajectories. Given a task described in natural language, our process produces a linear reward function in three steps: distill the task's objectives into a set of fundamental objectives and derive measurable outcome variables that capture those fundamental objectives, select a causally representative subset of outcome variables as the reward terms, and fit weights to those reward terms via preference elicitation. Our contributions describe the first step and formalize the latter two steps. The first is a guided workflow for deriving outcome variables. The second is a reduction of reward term selection to minimum-cost partial cover on a causal DAG, solved in polynomial time via max-flow. The third is a geometric framing of weight fitting as a convex feasibility problem iteratively narrowed by preference queries, solved by existing separation oracle methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first reward-design method that maintains a deterministically conflict-free feasible weight region, narrowed to a desired tolerance via a separation oracle with O(n log κ) preference queries.

</details>


### [346] [Redistribution-based Cost Inference Improves Sparse Safe Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12306)
*Ebenezer Gelo,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Steven James,Benjamin Rosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出红istribution-based Cost Inference (RCI)框架，将安全离线强化学习中的稀疏轨迹级停止反馈转化为稠密的每步成本标注，通过收益分解实现时序信用分配，从而训练约束离线策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全离线RL假设能够获得稠密的每步成本标注，但在实际应用中监督者通常只提供轨迹级别的停止反馈（即在第一个不安全转移处给出二元信号），缺乏每步的归因信息。这构成了一个时序信用分配问题，阻碍了约束策略的有效训练。

Method: 提出RCI框架：(1) 通过收益分解（return decomposition）将稀疏的轨迹级停止反馈转换为稠密的每步成本；(2) 在增强后的数据集上训练约束离线策略；(3) 理论上证明收益等价的重分布变换保持CMDP中可行策略集和最优拉格朗日乘子不变，即该变换在理论上是无损的，同时在实际中改善成本Critic学习的条件。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作实验中，RCI相比稀疏基线方法和基于分类器的方法显著降低了违规率，并且对异质数据集组成和标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RCI框架将稀疏停止反馈转化为稠密成本标注的理论无损性与实际收益相结合，为实际场景中的安全离线RL提供了一种有效的成本注释推断方法。

Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [347] [Predicting Mechanical Properties of Lignin-Containing Polyurethane Rigid Foams from Microstructure Using Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11447)
*Ilige S. Hage,Charbel Y. Seif,Jose Enrico Q. Quinsaat,Daniel J. van de Pas,Richard Vendamme,Walter Eeversd,Karolien Vanbroekhovend,Elias Feghalid*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究利用卷积神经网络（CNN）通过扫描电子显微镜（SEM）图像预测含木质素刚性聚氨酯（PU）泡沫的力学性能（密度、比压缩模量、比屈服强度和比压缩强度），实现了R²在0.850-0.91范围内的高精度预测，并通过Grad-CAM可视化证实网络关注细胞壁和支柱节点等物理相关区域，为非破坏性、数据驱动的泡沫材料性能评估提供了可解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物基刚性泡沫作为传统泡沫的可持续替代品性能良好，但其制造过程复杂且破坏性测试往往不切实际，因此需要探索一种无需破坏性测试即可评估力学性能的方法，通过机器学习将微观结构与力学性能关联起来。

Method: 研究采用定制双头卷积神经网络（CNN），利用SEM图像和对应的力学压缩数据为训练集，结合加权多输出损失函数优化网络，同时使用Grad-CAM可视化技术分析网络关注的微结构区域。实验中考察了不同类型和比例的木素基多元醇作为部分多元醇替代物。

Result: CNN在预测四种目标力学性能时表现出优异的性能：R²值范围为0.850-0.91，相关系数超过0.92，平均绝对误差百分比低于9%。Grad-CAM可视化显示网络将预测注意力集中在细胞壁和支柱节点等物理意义明确的微结构区域，确认了模型的可解释性。

Conclusion: 该CNN框架被证明是一种可解释、非破坏性、数据驱动的工具，能够有效预测和理解生物基PU泡沫的力学行为，从而减少因耗时的制造过程和破坏性测试带来的不便，为生物基刚性泡沫材料的性能评估提供了一种创新途径。

Abstract: Bio-based alternatives for conventional rigid foams have proven to be good substituents owing to their enhanced sustainability and competitive performance. However, because their manufacturing processes are complex and destructive testing is often impractical, this study investigates whether microstructural features can be correlated with mechanical properties in lignin-containing rigid polyurethane (PU) foams using machine-learning approaches. Various types and percentages of lignin-based polyols were investigated as a partial polyol replacement. Scanning electron microscopy (SEM) images and corresponding mechanical compression data were used to train a custom state-of-art dual-head convolutional neural network (CNN) targeting the specific prediction of density, specific compression modulus, specific yield strength, and specific compression strength. The CNN was optimized with a weighted multi-output loss function, achieving strong predictive performance R2 values ranging from 0.850 to 0.91 and correlation coefficients above 0.92 while maintaining mean absolute error percentages below 9%. This proves the trained network capability to predict and capture morphological features governing load bearing responses. On the other hand, Grad-CAM visualization revealed that the network focused its predictions on physically meaningful microstructural regions such as cell walls and strut junctions, which confirm that the proposed network can be classified as an interpretable, non-destructive and data-driven framework for predicting and understanding bio-based PU foams mechanical behavior, hence reducing the inconvenience caused by time consuming manufacturing and destructive testing.

</details>


### [348] [Stochastic Corridor Time Network Capacity Planning for Low Altitude Airspace Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11477)
*Yipu Yao,Li Ding,Yanlu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机物流运营商在低空空域走廊上预先预订容量的随机规划问题。针对走廊授权不可转让、按时间窗口预订且过期作废的特点，构建了两阶段随机规划模型，通过Benders分解和列生成求解，并用深圳案例验证了预订容量集中在中枢走廊、需求水平和预订价格主要影响容量数量而非走廊选择的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中美欧监管机构已将低空空域开放为按时间窗、提前预订、过期作废的走廊授权，这与传统外生空域容量模型不同。预订容量在网络中沿时变路径联合消耗且不可跨走廊/时间窗转移，现有模型无法刻画这种网络耦合，因此需要新的优化框架来回答"在需求实现前应预订多少容量"这一核心决策问题。

Method: 构建两阶段随机规划模型：第一阶段在需求实现前决策各走廊-时间单元的预订容量（扣除预订成本），第二阶段根据实现的请求在时间扩展网络上进行接受选择和路径路由（recourse）。证明弧段形式与路径打包形式等价，采用Benders分解求解LP松弛，子问题用列生成处理，并通过截断路径方法扩展到更大规模实例，所有预订和路由决策均恢复为整数方案。

Result: 在中等规模网络上取得个位数百分比的LP-Benders间隙，通过截断路径方法可扩展到更大规模实例。深圳案例显示预订容量集中于结构中枢走廊；需求水平和预订价格对预订容量的数量影响更大，而对走廊选择的影响相对较小。

Conclusion: 本文为无人机物流运营商在低空空域预订容量问题提供了首个考虑网络耦合的两阶段随机规划求解框架，揭示了网络中心性、需求水平和价格对容量预订策略的差异化影响，为实际空域容量预订决策提供了量化工具和管理洞见。

Abstract: Regulators in China, the United States, and the European Union now provide low-altitude airspace access as priced, time-windowed corridor authorizations, booked in advance and forfeited if unused. We ask how much capacity a UAV logistics planner should reserve on each corridor--time unit before demand is realized, to maximize expected profit net of reservation cost. Reserved capacity cannot be transferred across corridors or time windows and is consumed jointly along time-respecting paths, so reservations are coupled through the network in ways that models with exogenous airspace capacity cannot capture. We formulate a two-stage stochastic program whose recourse selects and routes accepted requests on a time-expanded network, prove its arc-based and path-packing forms equivalent, and solve it by Benders decomposition with column-generated subproblems. The decomposition operates on the LP relaxation, and all reported reservation and routing decisions are recovered as integer plans. Computational experiments achieve single-digit LP-Benders gaps on moderate-sized networks and extend to much larger instances through a truncated-path approach. A Shenzhen case study shows reservations concentrating on structurally central corridors, with demand level and reservation price having more influence on the quantity of capacity reserved than the selection of corridors.

</details>


### [349] [Comparative Analysis of Low-Rank Adaptation in Large Language Models versus Dense Embedding Regression for Headline Click-Through Rate Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11912)
*Samarth Sirsat,Anirudha Shinde,Amit Sethi,Aman Verma*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文比较了LoRA微调的因果语言模型（LOLAQwen，0.6B）与稠密嵌入回归模型在头条标题选择任务上的表现。嵌入回归模型Top-1准确率达42.79%，优于LoRA微调语言模型的35.70%，表明轻量级判别式方法在该任务上优于参数高效微调的小型生成式语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字内容标题的点击率优化是在线媒体和推荐系统中的重要问题。虽然大语言模型（LLM）在生成任务上表现出色，但其在判别式排序任务（如从候选集中选择最优标题）上的有效性尚不清晰。本文旨在系统比较两种不同范式的模型在标题选择任务上的表现，以填补这一研究空白。

Method: 本文将标题选择建模为赢家通吃（winner-take-all）的分类问题，使用包含3,263个A/B测试标题组的数据集，比较了两种方法：（1）LoRA参数高效微调的因果语言模型LOLAQwen（0.6B）；（2）稠密嵌入回归模型。评估指标为Top-1准确率，即模型正确识别出表现最佳标题的组数占比。

Result: 嵌入回归模型取得了42.79%的Top-1准确率，而LoRA微调的语言模型仅为35.70%，两者差距约7个百分点。

Conclusion: 对于头条标题选择这一任务，轻量级判别式方法（嵌入回归）能够超越经参数高效微调的小型生成式语言模型。这一发现凸显了嵌入回归模型作为生成式模型在吞吐量要求高的内容排序应用中的高效替代方案的潜力。

Abstract: Optimizing digital content headlines for click-through rate (CTR) is an important problem in online media and recommendation systems. While large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong generative capabilities, their effectiveness for discriminative ranking tasks, such as selecting the highest-performing headline from a set of candidates, remains less well understood. In this work, we compare a LoRA-fine-tuned causal language model, LOLAQwen (0.6B), with a dense embedding regression model for headline selection. We formulate headline selection as a winner-take-all classification problem and evaluate both approaches using a dataset of 3,263 A/B-tested headline groups. Performance is measured using Top-1 accuracy, defined as the proportion of groups for which the model correctly identifies the highest-performing headline. The embedding regression model achieves a Top-1 accuracy of 42.79%, compared with 35.70% for the LoRA-fine-tuned language model. These results indicate that, for this headline selection task, a lightweight discriminative approach can outperform a small generative language model fine-tuned using parameter-efficient adaptation. The findings highlight the potential of embedding-based regression models as efficient alternatives to generative models for high-throughput content ranking applications.

</details>


### [350] [An improved bond-associated peridynamic model and its adaptive coupling with CCM for fracture analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11950)
*Wenping Han,Bowen Sun,Shankun Liu,Fei Han*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文对键结合近场动力学（BAPD）模型中的修正因子进行了重新构造，基于应变能密度等效原理建立BAPD与经典连续介质力学（CCM）模型之间的关系，并在此基础上开发了BAPD-CCM耦合模型，通过引入"Morphing"函数实现两模型间的平滑过渡。数值算例表明该耦合模型在保持精度的同时大幅提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BAPD模型在实际应用中面临计算成本高的问题，需要一种既能保持断裂模拟能力又能提高计算效率的方法。通过能量密度等效重新构造修正因子可提升BAPD精度，而构建BAPD-CCM耦合模型则有望在关键区域使用BAPD、在其他区域使用高效的CCM模型，从而降低整体计算负担。

Method: （1）基于材料点处BAPD模型与CCM模型之间的应变能密度等效，重新推导BAPD的修正因子；（2）以FEM解为参考验证所提修正因子的精度提升效果；（3）基于能量密度等效原理建立BAPD-CCM耦合模型，引入Morphing函数实现两模型间平滑过渡；（4）时间域采用显式积分（适用于准静态和动态问题），空间上CCM用有限单元离散、BAPD用粒子离散；（5）通过L2范数、半H1范数和位移误差的能量范数评估耦合模型精度。

Result: 数值验证表明耦合模型具有更高的计算效率。在Mode I裂纹扩展问题中，计算成本相比纯BAPD模型降低了72%以上，且预测的裂纹扩展路径与实验结果吻合。精度上，通过与FEM解对比及多种误差范数评估验证了模型准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于能量密度等效的修正因子和BAPD-CCM耦合模型，在保持断裂模拟精度的同时显著降低了计算成本，为近场动力学在实际工程大规模断裂问题中的应用提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This paper reformulates the correction factor in the force-state of the bond-associated peridynamic (BAPD) model. The reformulation is established from the strain energy density equivalence between the BAPD model and the classical continuum mechanics (CCM) model at a material point. With the FEM solution taken as the reference, the proposed correction factor improves the accuracy of the BAPD solution in this study. Furthermore, a BAPD-CCM coupled model is developed based on the above energy density equivalence, and a ``Morphing" function is introduced to achieve a smooth transition between the two models. For time integration, explicit schemes are adopted for both quasi-static and dynamic problems. In the spatial discretization, the CCM model is discretized by elements, whereas the BAPD model is discretized by particles. The solution accuracy of the coupled model is validated by comparison with the FEM solution and by evaluating the $L^{2}$ norm, the $H^{1}_{\mathrm{semi}}$, and the energy norm of the displacement error. Two- and three-dimensional numerical examples show that the proposed model has higher computational efficiency. For example, in the Mode I crack propagation problem, its computational cost is reduced by more than 72\% compared with that of the pure BAPD model, and the predicted crack patterns agree with experimental results.

</details>


### [351] [Physics-Constrained Co-Optimization and Data-Driven Layer-Resolved Classification of a Hybrid CZT/PIPS Detector for Mixed Radiation Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12167)
*Renlong Jie,Fan Yang,Shouzhi Xi,Sanqi Tang,Wanqi Jie*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究一款紧凑型混合辐射探测器的优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型混合辐射仪器需要在低质量带电粒子入射窗口与高Z光子探测深度之间取得平衡。初始40×20×10 mm³的设计预算下，两种探测头方案（S1和S2）均无法同时满足灵敏度和分辨率指标。

Method: 通过硬约束搜索综合考量光子衰减与沉积、Hecht电荷收集、带电粒子能量损失、立体角、分辨率预算、时序及响应矩阵条件，对S1和S2方案进行比较。最终保留S2观测结构，放宽封装深度约束，将裸CZT分为独立偏置的五层，增加低噪声求和通道用于能谱分析，并采用层分辨梯度提升分类器进行混合场分析。

Result: 在U95电子学模型下，最终40×20×12.570 mm³探测头使用五层、每层180 V偏置的7.870 mm裸CZT,662 keV灵敏度为32.804 cps/(μSv/h)，独立求和分辨率为2.303% FWHM（95百分位），β/α绝对效率下界分别为35.679%/39.832%。方案满足原始探测性能标准。

Conclusion: 通过将裸CZT分层独立偏置并扩展封装深度至12.570 mm，可在不牺牲混合场分析能力的前提下，实现满足全部性能指标的紧凑混合辐射探测器设计。

Abstract: Compact mixed-radiation instruments must preserve a low-mass charged-particle entrance while providing enough high-Z depth for photon sensitivity. We first compare two detector heads within a 40 x 20 x 10 mm^3 design budget. S1 places bare CdZnTe (CZT) and passivated implanted planar silicon (PIPS) branches side by side and estimates three rates. S2 adds 0.50 mm of CZT behind PIPS to estimate X/gamma, low-beta, high-beta, and alpha rates. A hard-constraint search combines photon attenuation and deposition, Hecht charge collection, charged-particle energy loss, solid angle, resolution budgeting, timing, and response-matrix conditioning. Feasible screening points exist inside the initial envelope, but a fresh transport/electronics assessment gives only 25.04-25.07 cps/(uSv/h) under the robust H*(10) convention, and every conservative electronics draw exceeds 2.5% FWHM. We therefore retain the more informative S2 observation structure, relax only the 10 mm package-depth constraint, divide the bare CZT into independently biased layers, and add a low-noise sum channel for spectroscopy plus layer-resolved gradient-boosted classification for mixed-field analysis. The extension campaign contains 2.40 million Geant4 11.4.1 histories over three depths, five transport seeds, and 14 particle/energy cases. The final 40 x 20 x 12.570 mm^3 head uses 7.870 mm of bare CZT in five layers at 180 V per layer. Under the application-scenario U95 electronics profile, its live-time-corrected 662 keV sensitivity is 32.804 cps/(uSv/h), its independent-sum resolution is 2.303% FWHM at the 95th percentile, and the propagated beta/alpha absolute-efficiency lower bounds are 35.679%/39.832%. Additional endpoint transport covers 20 keV-3 MeV photons, 3-7 MeV alpha particles, and 155 keV-3.5 MeV beta spectra. The extended design therefore passes the original detector-performance criteria in the U95 model.
